We further fine-tune two of the best performing opensource models, i.e., Gemma3n and OmniLingual, using various methods. With this, we uncover systematic performance gaps tied to speaker role and gender, raising concerns about equitable deployment in clinical settings, which are further mitigated by fairness-aware fine-tuning. To this end, we propose SamaVaani, a unified debiasing technique that simultaneously improves ASR performance and improves fairness across demographic groups.

</details>


### [139] [GAVEL: Grounded Caption Error Verification and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26923)
*Zixian Gao,Atsushi Hashimoto,Kuniaki Saito*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出GAVEL任务，联合处理图文不一致的检测、解释和视觉证据定位，并构建数据集与基准，实验表明现有强大模型在此任务上表现困难，而监督训练可带来一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型常产生图文不对齐的幻觉输出，现有工作缺乏对错误检测、自然语言解释及视觉定位的联合处理，三者协同可提供更完整的错误分析。

Method: 定义GAVEL（Grounded Caption Error Verification and Localization）任务，涵盖验证、解释、定位三个子任务；构建人工标注数据集与评测基准；训练监督基线模型以评估该任务框架能否为上述能力提供可学习的监督信号。

Result: 即便是强大的闭源模型在GAVEL任务上仍然表现困难；在人工标注训练集上训练的监督基线模型，在定位和解释指标上均取得一致改善。

Conclusion: GAVEL任务及数据集有效填补了图文对齐错误联合分析的空白，监督学习可以系统提升模型在该任务上的表现，为未来研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) often produce hallucinated or inconsistent outputs, where text and images are not properly aligned. Addressing this issue requires not only detecting misalignment but also explaining the discrepancy and localizing its visual evidence. We introduce GAVEL (Grounded Caption Error Verification and Localization), a task that jointly addresses verification, explanation, and localization for image-text pairs. To support systematic evaluation, we also present a corresponding dataset and benchmark. We further train a supervised baseline on the human-annotated training split to assess whether GAVEL provides learnable supervision for these abilities. Experiments show that even strong closed-source models struggle on GAVEL, while the supervised baseline yields consistent improvements across grounding and explanation metrics.

</details>


### [140] [Term-Centric Hierarchy Induction from Heterogeneous Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26963)
*Elena Senger,Yuri Campbell,Jan-Peter Bergmann,Rob van der Goot,Barbara Plank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于术语（term-centric）的层次化分类法归纳框架，通过自动术语提取将异构语料映射到共享表示空间，结合领域先验与数据驱动聚类构建可解释的层次结构，在大规模多源文档基准上优于基于文本和摘要的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类法归纳方法依赖文档级表示，难以跨异构数据源泛化，且无法聚焦于与知识组织相关的特定领域概念，亟需一种可扩展至海量文档的跨源层次化知识组织方法。

Method: 提出以术语为中心的层次分类法归纳框架：（1）自动术语提取，将多源文档映射到共享表示空间，实现跨源对齐；（2）融合领域先验与数据驱动聚类，构建可解释的层次结构。

Result: 在包含超过100万篇文档的英德多源新基准上，该方法在跨源一致性和层次质量方面均优于基于文本和基于摘要的基线方法；此外，通过德国区域创新分析的案例研究展示了其在技术景观映射中的实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 以术语为中心的跨源层次分类法归纳方法能够有效整合异构大规模语料，生成高质量、可解释的知识层级结构，适用于政策分析、创新监测和技术领域映射等实际场景。

Abstract: Organizing knowledge from diverse text sources into interpretable hierarchies is crucial for tasks such as policy analysis, innovation monitoring, and exploratory domain mapping. Existing taxonomy induction methods typically rely on document-level representations that capture entire documents rather than the specific domain concepts relevant for knowledge organization, limiting their ability to generalize across heterogeneous sources. We propose a term-centric framework for inducing hierarchical taxonomies from heterogeneous corpora that scales to massive document collections. Our approach maps documents from diverse sources into a shared representation space using automatic term extraction, enabling robust cross-source alignment. Based on these representations, we construct interpretable hierarchies that integrate domain priors with datadriven clustering. Experiments on a novel English and German multi-source benchmark of over one million documents demonstrate that our method improves cross-source coherence and hierarchy quality over text- and summarybased baselines. A case study on German regional innovation analysis further demonstrates its practical utility for technology landscape mapping.

</details>


### [141] [RedVox: Safety and Fairness Gaps in Speech Models Across Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26968)
*Beatrice Savoldi,Sara Papi,Wafa Aissa,Matteo Negri,Luisa Bentivogli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RedVox是一个多语言音频语音安全与公平性基准，覆盖五种语言，评估发现现有语音模型在非英语语言和口语输入下安全漏洞加剧，且目前仅8%的模型发布文档包含多语言安全分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音模型在现实世界多语言场景中广泛部署，但其在非英语环境及自然条件下的安全性与公平性缺乏系统研究。当前模型发布的安全报告极少涵盖多语言分析，存在显著的研究空白。

Method: 作者首先调研了现有领先语音模型的安全报告实践；随后构建了RedVox基准，基于真实人声收集五种语言（英、法、意、西、德）的不安全与不公平刻板印象请求；在此基准上评估八个最先进模型；最后通过调查贡献数据的参与者，记录语音安全数据收集中的隐私与伦理挑战。

Result: （1）仅8%的语音模型发布文档包含多语言安全分析；（2）即使在非对抗性条件下，模型仍存在安全漏洞；（3）非英语语言的漏洞更为严重；（4）口语输入会放大风险；（5）收集真实语音数据面临独特的个人隐私挑战。

Conclusion: 语音模型在多语言和自然语音场景下的安全性问题被严重忽视，亟需建立系统的多语言安全评估标准，同时需关注语音安全研究中数据收集的社会技术挑战。

Abstract: Speech-capable models are increasingly deployed in real-world applications across languages. Yet their safety and fairness beyond English settings and under naturalistic conditions remain understudied. We survey safety reporting practices across state-of-the-art speech model releases, finding that only 8% document any multilingual analysis. To address this gap, we introduce RedVox, a multilingual safety and fairness benchmark for audio and speech built on real voices, covering unsafe and unfair stereotypical requests across five languages (English, French, Italian, Spanish, and German). Evaluating eight state-of-the-art models, we find that vulnerabilities persist even under non-adversarial conditions, worsen in non-English languages, and are amplified when the request comes from a spoken input. Finally, by surveying the participants who contributed to RedVox, we document the unique personal and privacy challenges of collecting speech data with human participants, pointing to broader sociotechnical challenges in naturalistic speech safety research.

</details>


### [142] [Auditing Framing-Sensitive Behavioral Instability in Large Language Models for Mental Health Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26982)
*Abla Bedoui,Ashley L. Greene,Mohammed Cherkaoui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在心理健康等敏感对话场景中，对语义相似但上下文框架不同的提示所表现出的行为变异，并通过层探测、激活引导等分析揭示了框架效应在模型内部表征中的编码方式，表明上下文鲁棒性应作为评估对话AI可信度的重要维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理健康支持等心理敏感应用中，大语言模型的行为稳定性与一致性对可信人机交互至关重要。然而，语义相似的关切可通过不同上下文框架呈现，可能引发不一致的模型响应。现有研究主要关注行为层面，对框架相关变异如何在模型内部表征中反映知之甚少。

Method: 采用受控匹配提示（controlled matched prompts），涵盖多种上下文框架条件，在多个指令微调模型家族上实验。使用层级探测分析（layer-wise probing）解码行为相关信息在Transformer各深度的编码强度；设置保留框架探测（held-out framing probes）与强词汇基线对比；并通过激活引导实验（activation steering）检验框架相关表征方向对下游行为的部分调控能力。

Result: 跨架构一致发现：上下文框架系统性改变模型的解释性响应倾向；行为相关信息在整个Transformer深度均可解码，但解码强度存在架构依赖差异；保留框架探测在所有架构上持续高于随机水平，且超越强词汇基线；激活引导实验证实框架相关表征方向可部分调节下游行为输出。

Conclusion: 对上下文变异的鲁棒性应作为评估心理健康导向对话AI系统一致性与可信度的重要考量维度，研究揭示了框架敏感性不仅是行为现象，更深层编码于模型内部表征之中。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being integrated into mental health support tools and other psychologically sensitive conversational applications. In such settings, behavioral stability and consistency are important for trustworthy human-AI interaction. However, semantically similar concerns can be presented through different contextual framings, potentially eliciting different model responses. Such framing-sensitive variability may challenge user expectations regarding system behavior and complicate the assessment of AI reliability. While prior studies have primarily examined such effects at the behavioral level, less is known about how framing-related variation is reflected in the internal representations of aligned language models. In this work, we investigate these effects using controlled matched prompts spanning multiple contextual framing conditions across several instruction-tuned model families. Across architectures, framing systematically alters interpretive response tendencies. Layer-wise probing analyses show that behavior-associated information remains decodable throughout transformer depth, with architecture-dependent variation in decoding strength. Moreover, held-out framing probes remained consistently above chance across architectures despite strong lexical baselines. Activation steering experiments further suggest that framing-associated representational directions can partially modulate downstream behavioral outcomes. Finally, these findings indicate that robustness to contextual variation may represent an important consideration when evaluating the consistency and trustworthiness of conversational AI systems deployed in mental-health-oriented interactions.

</details>


### [143] [ReaORE: Reasoning-Guided Progressive Open Relation Extraction Empowered by Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26986)
*Xin Lin,Liang Zhang,Guoqi Ma,Hongyao Tu,Jinsong Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReaORE 提出了一种由粗到精的关系推理框架，通过关系过滤和关系预测两阶段，增强开放关系抽取对未见关系的泛化能力，尤其提升了对易混淆关系的区分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前开放关系抽取的两类主流方法各有缺陷：聚类方法无法生成关系标签且泛化能力差；基于大语言模型直接生成标签的方法又缺乏足够判别力，难以区分易混淆关系。因此需要一种兼具强泛化与高判别力的方案。

Method: 提出 Reasoning-guided progressive OpenRE (ReaORE) 框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 关系过滤——从多维度推理理解关系和实例，生成初始关系集，并通过基于嵌入的相似度进行补充与过滤，确保目标关系被包含；(2) 关系预测——在候选关系集上通过细粒度对比推理预测目标关系，更好地区分易混淆关系。

Result: 在两个广泛使用的 OpenRE 数据集上的大量实验表明，ReaORE 优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: ReaORE 通过由粗到精的递进式推理框架，有效解决了开放关系抽取中泛化能力不足和易混淆关系难以区分的双重挑战。

Abstract: Open Relation Extraction (OpenRE) requires a model to extract unseen relations between head and tail entities from unstructured text for real-world applications. The core challenge of OpenRE lies in achieving reliable generalization to unseen relation types. Current OpenRE approaches either employ clustering techniques, which cannot generate relation labels and suffer from poor generalization, or rely on direct relation label generation via Large Language Models (LLMs), which lack sufficient discriminative capacity to distinguish easily confused relations. To address these limitations, we propose Reasoning-guided progressive OpenRE (ReaORE), a framework for performing relation extraction through coarse-to-fine relation reasoning. Specifically, ReaORE consists of two key stages: (i) relation filtering, which reasons over multiple aspects to understand relations and instances, yielding an initial relation set, and further supplements and filters relations via embedding-based similarity to ensure the target relation is included; (ii) relation prediction, which aims to predict the target relations from the above set via fine-grained comparative reasoning to better distinguish easily confused relations. Extensive experiments on two widely used OpenRE datasets demonstrate that ReaORE outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [144] [Where Do Models Find Happiness? Emotion Vectors in Open-Source LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26987)
*Sinie van der Ben,Raphaël Baur,Yannick Metz,Mennatallah El-Assady*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509和Gemma-4-E4B-it两个开源模型中复现了Claude Sonnet 4.5中发现的情感向量现象。两个模型均成功恢复了效价几何结构（PC1-效价相关性分别达r=0.76和r=0.83），但效价表征在模型深度上的涌现模式存在显著差异：Gemma早期编码、后期崩塌，Apertus则相反。唤醒度编码则依赖于提取语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证此前在Claude Sonnet 4.5中发现的情感向量（emotion vectors）是否具有跨模型和跨架构的普适性，并探索效价与唤醒度表征在不同开源模型中的几何结构与层深度分布规律。

Method: 使用两个模型生成的语料库，对Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509和Gemma-4-E4B-it所有层提取情感对比向量（emotion contrast vectors），通过主成分分析（PCA）分别计算PC1与效价、PC2与唤醒度的相关性，并分析表征沿模型深度的变化模式。

Result: （1）两个模型均恢复出效价几何结构，Gemma PC1-效价r=0.83，Apertus r=0.76，接近Claude的r=0.81；（2）效价表征的层深度模式截然相反：Gemma在早期层强编码、后期崩塌，Apertus早期缺失、中层涌现；（3）唤醒度编码受提取语料影响显著：Gemma生成故事下PC2-唤醒度相关性更高（r达0.45），Apertus生成故事下较弱（r≤0.21）。

Conclusion: 情感向量的效价几何结构具有跨模型普适性，但其在模型深度上的涌现模式依赖于模型架构；唤醒度表征则对语料分布更敏感。该研究开源了实验代码与数据集，以促进情感表征的可复现研究。

Abstract: Recent work identified emotion vectors in Claude Sonnet 4.5, which are internal representations that encode emotion concepts, causally influence behavior, and exhibit geometry mirroring human psychological structure. We test the generality of these findings in two open-weight models, Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509 and Gemma-4-E4B-it, extracting emotion contrast vectors across all layers, using two model-generated corpora. We recover valence geometry for both models, with peak PC1--valence correlations of $r = 0.76$ and $r = 0.83$, approaching the $r = 0.81$ reported for Claude.Beyond replication, we observe notable differences in how valence representations emerge across model depth. In Gemma-4-E4B-it, valence is strongly encoded in early layers but collapses towards later layers, whereas Apertus-8B-Instruct-2509 exhibits the opposite pattern, with valence representations absent in early layers, but emerging at mid depths. Arousal encoding, in contrast, is sensitive to the extraction corpus: both models show stronger PC2--arousal alignment with Gemma-generated stories ($r$ up to $0.45$) than Apertus-generated ones ($r \leq 0.21$), suggesting arousal-relevant cues are unevenly distributed across generated corpora. We open-source our experiment code and dataset for reproducible investigation of emotion representations across language model architectures.

</details>


### [145] [MinGram: A Minimalist Unigram Tokenizer with High Compression and Competitive Morphological Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27019)
*Sander Land*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MinGram 是一种极简化的 Unigram 分词器训练方法，它保留了 Unigram 的 token 列表表示，但用 BPE 种子词汇、Hard EM 最小 token 路径、单次扁平剪枝替代了传统 Unigram 复杂的后缀数组、前向-后向传递和迭代剪枝循环，在保持压缩率和下游质量的同时大幅简化了训练流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Unigram 分词器虽然具有优雅的可编辑词汇表表示，但其训练过程依赖后缀数组、前向-后向算法和迭代剪枝，计算沉重且复杂。作者希望保留 Unigram 的表示优势，同时将训练简化到接近分词器推理本身的复杂度。

Method: 提出 MinGram 方法：(1) 使用 BPE 派生的种子词汇初始化；(2) 在最小 token 数量路径上执行 Hard EM（硬期望最大化）；(3) 仅进行单次扁平分数剪枝（而非迭代剪枝）。以 token 数量为主要优化目标，Unigram 分数仅作为平局决胜。还提出一种面向压缩的变体。

Result: 在六种语言上，MinGram 压缩效果优于 BPE 和标准 Unigram；压缩导向变体在匹配最强 token 计数压缩器的同时，保持显著更高的形态对齐度。在下游语言模型训练中，Unigram 家族（MinGram 位列最优）在 bits-per-byte 指标上一致优于 BPE。

Conclusion: MinGram 证明，通过精心设计的简化（BPE 种子 + Hard EM + 单次剪枝），可以在不牺牲压缩率和下游质量的前提下，将 Unigram 训练变得极为轻量，同时 token 计数作为主目标比概率分数更有效。

Abstract: The Unigram tokenizer uses an elegant representation which makes it straightforward to edit vocabularies, but its training is comparatively heavy and complex. We introduce MinGram (Minimalist Unigram), which keeps the token-list representation but simplifies training using a BPE-derived seed vocabulary, Hard EM on a minimum-token path, and a single flat score-pruning step. This removes the suffix array, the forward-backward pass, and the iterative prune loop, leaving a procedure that requires little beyond tokenizer inference itself. By making token count the primary objective and using a Unigram score only as a tiebreak, MinGram keeps the compression of pure token-count methods while retaining much of the morphological alignment and downstream quality of probabilistic ones. Across six languages, MinGram compresses better than both BPE and standard Unigram, and a compression-oriented variant matches the strongest token-count compressors while retaining substantially higher morphological alignment. In controlled downstream language-model training, Unigram-family tokenizers, with MinGram among the best, consistently beat BPE in bits-per-byte.

</details>


### [146] [Improving General Role-Playing Agents via Psychology-Grounded Reasoning and Role-Aware Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27025)
*Zhenhua Xu,Dongsheng Chen,Jian Li,Yitong Lin,Zhebo Wang,Jiafu Wu,Yizhang Jin,Chengjie Wang,Meng Han,Yabiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Psy-CoT（心理学驱动的思维链框架）和RAPO（角色感知策略优化）两种方法，解决角色扮演代理的深度推理和训练优化问题。Psy-CoT将预应答推理分解为交互感知、心理共情和逻辑构建三步；RAPO则通过角色-词元互信息非对称加权梯度，防止模型利用奖励模型漏洞。实验表明两者在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前监督微调范式仅鼓励表面行为模仿，缺乏深层拟人化内部推理过程，导致分布外泛化能力差。此外，基于LLM的奖励模型下，通用短语和真正的角色特有短语获得相同梯度信号，导致模型学会"攻击"奖励模型而非真正提升角色保真度。

Method: 提出两个核心方法：(1) Psy-CoT：基于心理学的思维链框架，将应答前推理分解为三步——交互感知（理解情境）、心理共情（从角色画像出发产生情感/动机）、逻辑构建（组织响应）；(2) RAPO：角色感知策略优化，利用角色画像与生成词元之间的互信息对梯度进行非对称加权——在正优势下放大角色特有词元的梯度，在负优势下衰减之，从而抑制奖励攻击行为。

Result: 在CoSER、CharacterBench和CharacterEval三个基准上，Psy-CoT优于现有角色扮演思维链方法；RAPO在多种模型规模上持续超越GRPO。

Conclusion: Psy-CoT通过结构化心理推理使模型从角色画像动态思考，RAPO通过互信息加权梯度解决奖励攻击问题，两者结合显著提升了通用角色扮演代理的角色保真度和泛化能力。

Abstract: Building general-purpose role-playing agents that faithfully portray any character from a natural-language profile remains challenging. The dominant paradigm -- supervised fine-tuning -- encourages behavioral mimicry without deep, human-like internal thought processes, resulting in poor out-of-distribution generalization. Therefore, we propose \textbf{Psy-CoT}, a psychology-grounded chain-of-thought framework that decomposes pre-response reasoning into three role-specific steps -- \emph{Interaction Perception}, \emph{Psychological Empathy}, and \emph{Logical Construction} -- so that the model \emph{thinks dynamically} from the profile rather than merely mimicking surface patterns. While structured reasoning provides a foundation, it alone is insufficient; reinforcement learning is essential to further align the model with character fidelity. However, we observe that under LLM-based reward models, both generic phrases that hack the reward model and genuinely role-specific phrases receive identical gradient signals -- this hacking accumulates over training, misleading the model into treating both as equally optimal choices. To address this, we propose \textbf{Role-Aware Policy Optimization (RAPO)}, which uses profile--token mutual information to weight gradients asymmetrically -- amplifying role-specific tokens under positive advantage while attenuating them under negative advantage. Experiments on CoSER, CharacterBench, and CharacterEval demonstrate that Psy-CoT outperforms existing role-playing CoT methods, and RAPO consistently surpasses GRPO across multiple model scales.

</details>


### [147] [NuclearQAv2: A Structured Benchmark for Evaluating Domain-Science Competence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27047)
*Henry Shaowu Yuchi,Michal Kucer,Benjamin H. Sims,Selma Peterson,Emily Taylor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了NuclearQAv2基准，包含约1240道核工程问答对，涵盖布尔、数值和口头三类，评估发现LLM在事实性问题上表现良好，但在定量推理和概念理解上仍面临巨大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在通用任务上表现出色，但在核工程等高度技术领域中，问题求解需要事实知识、定量推理和概念理解的综合能力。现有评估缺乏针对该领域的系统性基准，因此需要构建专项评测框架以衡量LLM在技术领域的可靠性。

Method: 提出NuclearQAv2基准，包含约1240个问答对，涉及布尔、数值和口头三类题型。采用混合流水线构建：结合专家编写问题、已有数据集，以及从领域专用技术语料库中通过LLM辅助生成问题。使用结构化提示实现自动化问题生成与回答评估，支持可扩展的基准构建与评测。

Result: 在多个LLM上评测发现，模型在事实性问题（布尔类）上表现普遍较好，但在定量推理（数值类）和概念理解（口头类）方面准确率显著下降，不同任务类型之间性能差异明显。

Conclusion: 多维度评测框架对于评估LLM在技术领域的能力至关重要。NuclearQAv2作为可扩展的基准，揭示了当前LLM在核工程领域定量推理与概念理解方面的不足，为未来模型改进提供了方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong performance across a wide range of tasks, but ensuring their reliability in highly technical domains remains a significant challenge. In nuclear engineering, problem solving often requires not only factual knowledge but also quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding. To address the need for systematic evaluation in this domain, we introduce NuclearQAv2, a benchmark for assessing LLMs on nuclear engineering knowledge. The benchmark comprises approximately 1,240 question-answer pairs spanning three categories: boolean, numeric, and verbal. NuclearQAv2 is constructed using a hybrid pipeline that combines expert-authored questions, existing datasets, and LLM-assisted generation from domain-specific technical corpora. By leveraging structured prompting for both automated question generation and response evaluation, the proposed framework enables scalable benchmark construction and evaluation. We evaluate a diverse set of LLMs using NuclearQAv2 and observe substantial performance differences across task types. While the models generally perform well on factual questions, quantitative reasoning and conceptual understanding remain considerably more challenging. These results highlight the importance of multi-faceted evaluation frameworks and establish NuclearQAv2 as a scalable benchmark for assessing LLM capabilities in technical domains.

</details>


### [148] [Towards Explainable Adjudicative Variance: Quantifying Judicial Discretion via Gated Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27069)
*Stanisław Sójka,Felix Steffek,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种法官感知门控多任务学习架构，通过引入细粒度结果分类体系作为结构性正则化，将案件客观事实与裁判背景（法官身份）进行解耦建模。在UK Employment Tribunal数据集上，该方法以远少于生成式微调基线的参数量取得了最优性能，尤其在模糊和稀有类别上提升显著，同时具备可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律结果预测需要区分客观案件事实和裁判背景——基于案情的裁决依赖事实证据，而程序性处置可能取决于法官的自由裁量。现有生成式方法将法官身份和分类体系强行注入单一自回归通道，导致两种上下文信号仅产生弱组合，未能有效解耦这两类信息。

Method: 提出Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning架构：(1) 引入细粒度结果分类体系作为编码器的监督信号，通过结构化正则化解耦不同的语义通路；(2) 设计Gated Fusion机制，基于细粒度法律课程动态调节对法官身份的依赖程度；(3) 使用LoRA适配的Gemma-4编码器与门控架构耦合。对比基线为Gemma-4 26B-A4B的监督微调，其中法官身份和分类体系以prompt token或自回归输出目标形式注入。

Result: 在13,937条UK Employment Tribunal判决上评估：(1) 该方法树立了新的最优基准；(2) 可训练参数量比生成式SFT基线少一个数量级；(3) 性能增益集中在最模糊和最稀有的结果类别上；(4) 学习到的法官嵌入和校准曲线可定位裁判背景驱动预测的具体案例，具备可解释性。

Conclusion: 在身份条件的法律结果分类中，条件化接口的选择比模型规模更重要：可微分的结构化组合（门控架构）比基于prompt的组合（在更大骨干模型上）产生更准确、更参数高效的模型。

Abstract: Legal outcome prediction must disentangle objective case facts from adjudicative context. Merit-based rulings rely on factual evidence while technical disposals may hinge on judicial discretion. We propose a Judge-Aware Gated Multi-Task Learning architecture that explicitly models this distinction. We introduce a fine-grained outcome taxonomy to supervise the encoder, enforcing a structural regularization that disentangles distinct semantic pathways. This granular legal curriculum enables our Gated Fusion mechanism to dynamically modulate reliance on judge identity. We evaluate our approach on 13,937 UK Employment Tribunal decisions. We benchmark our design against supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a Gemma-4 26B-A4B backbone, in which judge identity and the taxonomy are injected as prompt tokens or autoregressive output targets. The two contextual signals compose only weakly when forced through a single autoregressive channel. In contrast, coupling a LoRA-adapted Gemma-4 encoder with our gated architecture defines a new state of the art on this benchmark while requiring an order of magnitude fewer trainable parameters than the generative SFT baselines, with gains concentrated on the most ambiguous and rarest outcome classes. Beyond accuracy, the architecture is interpretable; learned judge embeddings and calibration profiles localize the cases where adjudicative context drives the prediction. These results indicate that, for identity-conditioned classification of legal outcomes, the choice of conditioning interface dominates scale: differentiable structured composition yields more accurate, more parameter-efficient models than prompt-based composition over a substantially larger backbone.

</details>


### [149] [The Riddle Riddle: Testing Flexible Reasoning in Large Language Models and Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27103)
*Bella Fascendini,Kathryn McGregor,Max D. Gupta,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"谜语式谜语"（riddle riddle）范式，通过将经典谜语改写为仅需字面解释即可回答的问题，检验LLM是基于表面形式（模式匹配）还是基于内容进行灵活推理。结果表明，人类与LLM在两种谜语类型上表现相反：LLM擅长真谜语但易对riddle riddles过度使用创造性推理（错误率90.8%），人类则相反。这说明LLM的表现更可能源于记忆检索而非真正的灵活策略选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM在许多认知任务上表现优异，但不清楚其准确性究竟来自训练数据的模式匹配，还是真正的灵活推理能力。需要一种能够区分"表面形式驱动"与"内容驱动"推理的实验范式。

Method: 设计"riddle riddle"范式：将流行谜语改写，使其答案仅需字面解释即可得出，但保留谜语的形式结构。若模型受表面特征驱动，会错误地采用创造性推理；若基于内容推理，则能灵活切换策略。实验涉及9个最先进LLM和100名人类参与者，比较两者在真谜语与riddle riddles上的表现及错误类型。

Result: LLM在真谜语上准确率（84.9%）远高于riddle riddles（50.7%）；人类则相反（真谜语50.5%，riddle riddles 80.5%）。错误分析显示，LLM在riddle riddles上90.8%的错误源于不当使用创造性推理，而人类在真谜语上仅57.6%的错误源于过度依赖字面推理。LLM的推理错误比例显著高于人类。

Conclusion: LLM在真谜语上的强劲表现更可能反映记忆检索而非灵活策略选择。若无专门设计用于揭示这种对比的刺激材料，容易将LLM看似推理的输出误认为真正的推理能力。

Abstract: Humans flexibly adapt their reasoning strategies to the requirements of a given problem. Large language models (LLMs) have performed well on many cognitive tasks, however, it is unclear whether this accuracy is a result of pattern matching from training data or flexible reasoning. Here, we introduce a novel paradigm to test this question: the riddle riddle paradigm. Riddle riddles are word problems written to mimic popular riddles, but altered so their answers only require literal interpretations. Identifying correct answers requires looking past the structure of each question and flexibly apply different reasoning strategies based on the content. If LLMs respond to surface features, such as form, a riddle-like structure should cause models to use an inventive reasoning strategy even when a literal interpretation suffices. Alternatively, if LLMs reason based on content, they should flexibly switch strategies when appropriate. Across two experiments with nine state-of-the-art LLMs and 100 human participants, we show humans and LLMs fail on this paradigm in opposite directions. LLMs were far more accurate on genuine riddles than on riddle riddles (84.9% vs. 50.7%); whereas humans showed the reverse effect (50.5% vs. 80.5%). Error analysis shows that 90.8% of LLM errors on riddle riddles (the condition where they show diminished performance) were due to inappropriate use of inventive reasoning while only 57.6% of human errors on genuine riddles were due to overextending literal reasoning. Thus, while both groups make mistakes, reasoning mistakes are made more often by LLMs than by humans. Overall, LLMs' strong performance on genuine riddles may reflect memory retrieval rather than flexible strategy selection, and without stimuli designed to elicit this contrast, it becomes easy to conflate LLM-generated outputs that look like reasoning with genuine reasoning.

</details>


### [150] [Forecasting With LLMs: Improved Generalization Through Feature Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27199)
*Humzah Merchant,Bradford Levy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用稀疏自编码器分析LLM在预测任务中的内部表征，识别出时间感知特征和前瞻偏差特征。通过对时间感知特征进行因果干预（放大），可显著减少预测中的前瞻偏差，同时保持通用推理性能，而直接操控前瞻偏差特征则无效。这表明可解释的时间特征能因果性地引导LLM进行更基于历史的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测任务要求模型基于历史模式进行泛化，但LLM可能存在"前瞻偏差"（look-ahead bias）——即不恰当地利用未来信息。研究者希望理解LLM内部是否依赖时间特异性知识还是可泛化模式，并探究能否通过特征干预来减少这种偏差，使模型更符合历史 grounded 的推理方式。

Method: 1）将LLM应用于多种预测任务；2）使用稀疏自编码器（sparse autoencoders）分析模型内部状态，识别时间感知（time-aware）特征和前瞻偏差（look-ahead-biased）特征；3）在完全不同的领域进行特征干预实验：分别放大时间感知特征和操控前瞻偏差特征，观察对预测偏差和通用推理性能的影响。

Result: 1）稀疏自编码器成功识别出与时间感知推理和前瞻偏差推理相关的可解释特征；2）放大时间感知特征能显著减少预测中的前瞻偏差，同时保持通用推理性能不下降；3）直接操控候选的前瞻偏差特征则未产生显著效果。

Conclusion: 可解释的时间特征可以被用于因果性地引导LLM朝向更基于历史的推理方式，这为理解和控制语言模型中的时序推理偏差提供了新方法，也对构建更可靠的预测系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Successful forecasting involves identifying patterns between historical and future states of the world which generalize to future observations. We apply LLMs to a variety of forecasting tasks and inspect their internal states using sparse autoencoders to understand whether they appear to rely on time-specific pieces of knowledge versus generalizable patterns. Our analyses identify features associated with both time-aware reasoning and look-ahead-biased reasoning. We then apply the LLMs to an entirely different domain and intervene on these features. We find that amplifying time-awareness features substantially reduces look-ahead bias on forecasting prompts while preserving general reasoning performance. In contrast, steering the candidate look-ahead-bias features does not produce an effect. These results suggest that interpretable temporal features can be used to causally shift LLMs toward more historically grounded reasoning.

</details>


### [151] [Syntactic Belief Update as the Driver of Garden Path Processing Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27206)
*Alan Zhou,Miloš Stanojević,John T. Hale*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"句法信念更新"的新指标，用于预测花园路径句的处理难度。该指标仅基于句法结构的概率分布更新幅度（用广义Rényi散度度量），而不依赖词汇概率，相比传统的词汇意外度能更好地拟合人类阅读时间数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 词汇意外度通常能很好地预测句子加工难度，但在花园路径句上表现不佳。研究者需要一种能够捕捉句法再分析过程的替代指标，以解释人类在花园路径句关键词语处的加工困难。

Method: 提出"句法信念更新"方法：处理器主动维护一个关于句法树的概率分布（句法信念），并在每个新词出现后更新该分布。当花园路径被打破时，句法信念需要大幅修正，更新幅度通过广义Rényi散度来量化。该度量依赖于词汇项触发的句法更新，但完全独立于词汇项本身的概率。

Result: 句法信念更新指标在花园路径句的人类阅读时间数据上，比词汇意外度提供了更好的拟合效果。

Conclusion: 该研究为心理语言学开辟了一个新方向：探索纯粹非词汇的、基于句法的替代指标来解释句子加工难度，而非依赖传统的词汇意外度。

Abstract: Garden path sentences present a processing difficulty for humans -- the sentence prefix leads the listener towards one interpretation, until the listener hears a critical word that shows that the initial interpretation was wrong. Lexical surprisal, a measure that usually predicts sentence processing difficulty quite well, fails to provide good predictions for garden path sentences.
  We propose an alternative that actively predicts a probability distribution over syntactic trees (its syntactic belief) and updates that distribution after each new word. If a processor is led down a garden path, syntactic beliefs will be wrong and will require a large update at the critical word. The magnitude of the update is measured with a generalized Rényi divergence. Crucially, this metric is dependent on lexical items, but is fully independent of the probability of lexical items. This Syntactic Belief Update provides a better fit to the human reading time data on garden path sentences. This suggests a new research direction examining purely non-lexical alternatives to surprisal for psycholinguistics.

</details>


### [152] [Paved with True Intents: Intent-Aware Training Improves LLM Safety Classification Across Training Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27210)
*Jeremias Ferrao,Niclas Müller-Hof,Iustin Sîrbu,Traian Rebedea,Yftah Ziser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出安全分类器应将用户意图作为提示与最终标签之间的显式信号进行建模，并构建了AIMS数据集（1,724条带意图描述和危害标签的困难安全提示），通过多种训练范式验证了意图感知训练的有效性，其中GRPO奖励意图忠实度的方法在五个外部安全基准上取得最强平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前安全分类器直接根据提示预测危害标签，忽略了用户意图这一中间信号。作者认为，显式建模用户意图可以提供更紧凑、高质量的监督信号，从而提升安全分类器的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: （1）构建AIMS数据集：收集1,724条困难安全提示，人工标注意图描述和危害标签；（2）在监督微调（SFT）、偏好学习（DPO）、推理蒸馏和强化学习（GRPO）四种训练范式下，系统评估意图感知训练的效果；（3）在五个外部安全基准上测试模型性能与推理延迟，构建Pareto前沿进行对比。

Result: （1）基于模型生成意图错误的DPO训练优于SFT；（2）意图条件蒸馏在多数师生对中优于纯推理蒸馏；（3）使用GRPO直接奖励意图忠实度的方法在五个外部安全基准上取得最强平均性能；（4）意图感知模型构成了推理延迟-F1的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 忠实建模用户意图是一种紧凑且高质量的监督信号，能够训练出更鲁棒的安全分类器。AIMS虽规模小，但在多种训练范式下都能产出有竞争力的安全分类器，验证了意图感知方法的有效性。

Abstract: We argue that safety classifiers should model user intent as an explicit signal between the prompt and the final label. To study this, we introduce AIMS, a human-annotated dataset of 1,724 difficult safety prompts, each paired with an intent description and harm label. We use AIMS to evaluate intent-aware training across supervised fine-tuning, preference learning, reasoning distillation, and reinforcement learning. Despite its size, AIMS enables competitive safety classifiers across training regimes: DPO from model-generated intent errors improves over SFT, and intent-conditioned distillation outperforms reasoning-only distillation in most teacher-student pairs. Most notably, directly rewarding intent faithfulness with GRPO yields the strongest average performance across five external safety benchmarks, while our intent-aware models form the inference latency-F1 Pareto frontier. These results show that faithful intent modeling is a compact, high-quality supervision signal for more robust safety classifiers.

</details>


### [153] [Compositionality and the lexicon in evolutionary semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27228)
*Fausto Carcassi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将形式语义学的组合性原则融入进化建模的框架，让词汇意义与组合函数在概念简洁性和交际准确性的压力下协同演化。应用于量化语义演化，发现保守性这一著名语义共相作为高效的系统级抽象涌现出来，并调和了量化词可学性与既有进化模型之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有语义共相研究存在两极分化：要么建模固定信号结构的词汇，要么进行整体式组合而缺乏可解释的词汇部件，未能整合形式语义学中"句子意义由词汇意义递归组合而来"这一核心洞见。作者旨在填补这一空白，将组合性原则系统性地引入进化建模。

Method: 作者构建了一个协同演化框架，使词汇意义与组合函数在概念简洁性（simplicity）和交际准确性（accuracy）双重选择压力下共同演化。通过分析Pareto前沿，考察量化语义的演化动态，并关注句法结构的敏感性。

Result: 在Pareto前沿上，保守性（conservativity）作为高效的系统级抽象自然涌现。该框架对句法结构敏感，能够调和量化词可学性的经验证据与先前进化模型之间的冲突。

Conclusion: 形式语义学发展出的句子意义图景可以有效地与进化建模相结合。该框架为研究涉及语法范畴内全局压缩、句法论元的语义特化，以及词汇与组合意义协同演化的语言共相提供了可推广的模板。

Abstract: Formal semantics has shown that sentence meanings arise by recursively composing lexical meanings, yet much of the literature on semantic universals models either lexicons with fixed signal structures or holistic composition without interpretable lexical parts. We introduce a framework that integrates this fundamental insight of formal semantics in evolutionary modeling, by allowing lexical meanings and a composition function to co-evolve under pressures for conceptual simplicity and communicative accuracy. We apply this framework to the evolution of quantificational meaning. Analyzing the Pareto frontier, we find that the most well-known semantic universal, conservativity, emerges as an efficient system-wide abstraction. The account is sensitive to syntactic structure and helps reconcile tensions between empirical evidence on quantifier learnability and prior evolutionary models. More broadly, the results demonstrate that the picture of sentential meaning developed in formal semantics can be productively combined with evolutionary modeling. The framework offers a template for studying universals that involve global compression within a grammatical category, semantic specialization of syntactic arguments, and the co-evolution of lexical and compositional meaning.

</details>


### [154] [CARVE: Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency for Chunk-Parallel Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27229)
*Sayak Dutta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CARVE 提出内容感知门控机制，将擦除操作限制在 key 轴上，利用已写入 GPU 的循环输出张量作为免费的擦除门内容信号，并用每头单标量替代按值写门投影，在 1.3B 参数/100B tokens 规模上全面超越 GDN-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的 delta 规则架构 GDN-2 存在三个耦合缺陷：(1) 擦除门是"记忆盲"的——只看到到达的 token，不感知当前存储内容；(2) 值轴擦除掩码在值投影规模上浪费参数；(3) 值轴擦除在数学上破坏了 WY 形式三角形块求解器的有效性，阻碍了与 Transformer 竞争所需的训练效率。这三个问题源于同一个根因：擦除操作发生在错误的轴上。

Method: CARVE 的核心原则是：仅在 key 轴上执行擦除（可证明是 WY 求解器有效的充要条件）。在此框架内：(1) 重用已写入 GPU 显存的循环输出张量作为擦除门的内容信号（零额外存储开销）；(2) 将按值的写门投影替换为每头单个标量，大幅减少参数；(3) 初始化时与 GDN-2 逐比特等价，所有质量差异完全来自内容门的学习。论文提供了六条形式化定理，涵盖记忆容量、Lyapunov 稳定性、梯度流、表达力分离、Pareto 最优块大小及混合最优性。

Result: 在 1.3B 参数/100B tokens 训练规模下：WikiText 困惑度 15.72（比 GDN-2 降低 0.18，4.5σ 显著效应）；在 9 项常识推理基准上领先所有循环基线；在所有 RULER 检索探针上达到 SOTA；仅增加 0.4% 吞吐开销，峰值内存降低 13%，参数量减少 19%。

Conclusion: 将擦除操作限制在 key 轴是解决 GDN-2 三类缺陷的充分且必要设计选择。CARVE 通过内容感知门控和值效率设计，在不增加实质计算开销的前提下显著提升了循环模型的语言建模、推理和检索能力，同时减少了参数和内存占用。

Abstract: Recurrent models must forget in order to remember, yet the state of the art decides what to erase without consulting what is stored -- the gate sees only the arriving token, not the memory it is about to modify. This memory-blind gating is one of three coupled defects in the leading delta-rule architecture (GDN-2): the value-axis erase mask wastes parameters at the scale of the value projection, and -- as we prove -- mathematically prevents the WY-form triangular chunk solver that makes recurrent training competitive with Transformers.
  We introduce CARVE (Content-Aware Recurrent with Value Efficiency), which resolves all three problems through one principle: erase only on the key axis. This is provably necessary and sufficient for the WY-form solver to remain valid. Within it, CARVE reuses the recurrent output tensor -- already written to GPU memory -- as a free content signal for the erase gate, and replaces the per-value write-gate projection with a single scalar per head. At initialisation CARVE is bit-identical to GDN-2; any quality difference emerges from what the content gate learns.
  At 1.3B parameters trained on 100B tokens, CARVE achieves WikiText perplexity 15.72 (minus 0.18 vs. GDN-2, a 4.5-sigma effect), leads every recurrent baseline on nine common-sense reasoning benchmarks, and sets state of the art on every RULER retrieval probe -- at 0.4% throughput overhead, 13% lower peak memory, and 19% fewer parameters. Six formal theorems cover memory capacity, Lyapunov stability, gradient flow, expressivity separation, Pareto-optimal chunk size, and hybrid optimality.

</details>


### [155] [Bridging Talk and Thought: Understanding Dialogue Dynamics Across Collaborative Problem-Solving Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27233)
*Zhengyuan Liu,Stella Xin Yin,Min-Yen Kan,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于分析协作问题解决中对话的概念框架，采用双层编码方案整合认知/非认知问题解决与元认知调节机制，并在九个跨领域数据集上验证了其有效性与泛化能力，揭示了元认知调节是深层协作的关键区分因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能系统成为能够自主推理和战略合作的主动主体，理解和评估人机及多智能体协作中的对话互动变得至关重要。然而，现有的分析方法存在关键局限性，缺乏将认知、非认知与元认知维度整合起来的统一框架，难以有效捕捉协作问题解决中对话的复杂动态。

Method: 提出一个概念性分析框架，核心是一个层次化的双层编码方案（hierarchical two-layer coding scheme）：第一层编码认知性与非认知性问题解决行为，第二层编码元认知调节机制。该框架将两个层面有机整合，用以全面刻画协作对话中的互动模式。

Result: 在九个涵盖多个领域的数据集上验证了框架的有效性和泛化能力。分析结果揭示了人类与智能体如何协调知识、技能和努力来解决复杂问题，特别发现元认知调节可以作为区分深层协作与表层协作的关键判别因素。

Conclusion: 该框架为协作问题解决中的对话分析提供了系统化的方法论工具，能够有效弥合现有分析方法的不足。元认知调节在协作质量评估中扮演核心角色，为优化人机协作和多智能体系统的对话设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: We present a conceptual framework for analyzing dialogue in collaborative problem-solving contexts, with an emphasis on the emerging dynamics of human-AI and multi-agent collaboration. As intelligent systems become active agents capable of autonomous reasoning and strategic cooperation, understanding the dialogic interaction during collaborative problem solving is increasingly important for optimizing and evaluating such partnerships. Our framework addresses key limitations in current analytical approaches through a hierarchical two-layer coding scheme that integrates cognitive and non-cognitive problem solving with metacognitive regulatory mechanisms. We demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability across nine datasets spanning multiple domains, and provide insights into how humans and agents coordinate their knowledge, skills, and efforts to solve complex problems, showing in particular that metacognitive regulation can be an essential discriminator of deeper collaboration.

</details>


### [156] [LMs as Task-Specific Knowledge Bases: An Interpretability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27237)
*Amit Elhelo,Amir Globerson,Mor Geva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 语言模型以任务特定而非任务通用的方式编码事实知识，同一事实在不同任务中依赖不同的参数子集，挑战了"模型参数即知识库"的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识库的核心特性是：对同一事实的不同查询应返回一致结果，依赖单一真值源。本文旨在探究语言模型是否满足这一特性，从而检验"模型参数可视为知识库"这一流行观点的有效性。

Method: 采用行为分析和机制分析相结合的方法：(1) 行为层面，追踪训练过程中同一事实在不同任务上的涌现时间一致性；(2) 机制层面，通过参数定位实验识别不同任务对应的事实知识所依赖的参数子集；(3) 进一步分析思维链推理所调用的参数与评估任务参数之间的关系。

Result: 行为上，同一事实在某一任务上习得后，往往无法在训练过程中同步出现在其他任务上；机制上，同一事实在不同任务中依赖截然不同的参数子集；思维链推理的部分有效性来源于调用了超出评估任务本身的、任务特定的参数。

Conclusion: 模型"知道什么"与"如何被提问"在参数空间中紧密交织，这一发现动摇了"语言模型参数即知识库"的类比，并对事实知识的可靠性和可控性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) capture large amounts of factual knowledge applicable to a wide range of tasks, motivating the view of their parameters as a knowledge base. An important property of knowledge bases is that different queries for the same fact return consistent results, drawing on a single source of truth. We investigate whether LMs satisfy this property through behavioral and mechanistic analyses. Our results suggest that they encode knowledge in a task-specific manner. Behaviorally, facts acquired on one task frequently fail to co-emerge on others during training. Parameter localization experiments suggest a mechanistic explanation, revealing distinct parameter subsets underlying different tasks for the same fact. Finally, we show that chain-of-thought reasoning draws part of its effectiveness from engaging task-specific parameters beyond those tied to the evaluation task. Our findings suggest that what the model knows and how it is asked are intertwined in parameter space, undermining the "knowledge base" analogy and carrying implications for the reliability and controllability of factual knowledge in LMs.

</details>


### [157] [How Surprising Is Historical Italian to Language Models? Tokenization Tax, Comprehension Tax, and a Simple Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27275)
*Maria Levchenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个诊断框架，将LLM处理历史文本的困难分解为四个维度：分词成本、预测不确定性（惊异度）、语义鲁棒性和上下文敏感性。通过对17世纪意大利语、19世纪意大利语和18世纪俄语三个跨世纪数据集的评估，发现编码成本与理解能力存在分离——俄语和早期意大利语的分词惩罚相当，但预测难度差异显著；同时嵌入相似度在所有数据集上保持稳健（>0.85），且简单的时间上下文提示可降低约60%的历史惊异度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究将历史文本对LLM的困难视为单一障碍，混淆了正字法变异、语言距离和预训练暴露度等多种因素，缺乏系统性的分解分析框架。

Method: 提出四维诊断框架（分词成本、惊异度、语义鲁棒性、上下文敏感性），并在三个数据集上进行评估：新策展的17世纪意大利语语料库（1610-1689）、19世纪《约婚夫妇》作为高暴露度对照组、18世纪俄语民用印刷书籍作为正字法压力测试。

Result: 俄语和早期现代意大利语分词膨胀率相似（25-30%），但17世纪意大利语的惊异度是现代意大利语的2.4倍（学术散文达3.2倍），俄语仅适度增加；嵌入相似度在所有数据集上保持稳健（>0.85）；时间上下文提示可将历史惊异度降低约60%。

Conclusion: 历史文本对LLM施加一致的编码税，但数字图书馆可安全部署LLM用于语义检索任务；生成类应用则需谨慎适配。时间上下文提示是一种简单有效的模型无关缓解策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly critical to digital library workflows, yet their ability to process historical language remains poorly understood. Historical difficulty is typically treated as a monolithic barrier, conflating orthographic variation, linguistic distance, and pretraining exposure. In this paper, we propose a diagnostic framework that decomposes this difficulty into four distinct dimensions: tokenization cost, predictive uncertainty (surprisal), semantic robustness, and context sensitivity.
  We evaluate this framework on three datasets spanning three centuries: (1) a newly curated corpus of 17th-century Italian texts (1610-1689) digitized from original page images; (2) canonical 19th-century Italian "I Promessi Sposi" serving as a high-exposure control; and (3) 18th-century Russian civil print books as a contrastive orthographic stress test.
  Our results reveal a distinct dissociation between encoding cost and comprehension. While Russian and early modern Italian incur comparable tokenization penalties (25-30% inflation), their predictive difficulty diverges sharply. 17th-century Italian is on average 2.4 times more surprising than its modern equivalent - with academic prose reaching 3.2 times - whereas Russian shows only a modest increase. But predictive uncertainty does not imply representational degradation: embedding similarity remains robust (> 0.85) across all datasets, confirming that models can represent historical meaning even when generation is unstable.
  Finally, we demonstrate that a minimal temporal context prompt reduces historical surprisal by approximately 60%, offering a simple, model-agnostic mitigation. These findings suggest that while historical text imposes a consistent encoding tax, digital libraries can safely deploy LLMs for semantic retrieval tasks, provided that generative applications are carefully adapted.

</details>


### [158] [Multilingual Reasoning Cascades Need More Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27306)
*Arnav Mazumder,Dengjia Zhang,Shuyue Stella Li,Yulia Tsvetkov,Niyati Bafna*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种上下文感知的翻译级联方法，在最终翻译阶段保留原始问题、英文翻译及推理轨迹作为上下文，有效减少多语言推理中因信息丢失导致的错误传播，在多个基准测试中显著提升开放生成任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统翻译级联（翻译→推理→回译）在多语言推理中虽具有竞争力，但各阶段存在结构性信息损失，尤其丢失了文化背景、语域和消歧等关键线索。作者旨在通过简单且无需训练的方式弥补这一缺陷。

Method: 提出上下文感知翻译级联（context-aware translation cascade），在最终回译模块的上下文中额外提供：(1)原始语言问题，(2)英文翻译后的问题，(3)英文推理轨迹。该方法无需额外训练，可直接应用于现有流程。

Result: 在9个多语言基准（涵盖多种任务类型）、3个骨干模型、285种高/中/低资源语言上评估，开放生成任务在所有模型和资源条件下均取得显著提升。消融实验表明，原始语言问题携带了大部分有益上下文信息。

Conclusion: 研究强调了在机器翻译级联中优化信息流以缓解错误传播的必要性，并提供了一个简单可行的默认策略：将原始用户问题保留至流水线末端。

Abstract: Translation cascades for reasoning translate the query from another language to English, reason in English, and translate the answer back to the original language. This is a competitive approach to multilingual reasoning, but structurally lossy, since each stage discards information later stages may need, including cues for cultural grounding, register, and disambiguation. We examine the benefits of a simple and training-free intervention: a context-aware translation cascade, which additionally provides the original question, the English translated question, and the reasoning trace to the context of the final translation module. We evaluate gains across nine multilingual benchmarks including various task types, three backbone models, and 285 high-, mid-, and low-resource languages, and demonstrate strong gains for open-ended generation across models and resource regimes. We show that the original language question carries most of the beneficial context. Our study emphasizes the need to better design information flow in machine translation cascades for mitigating error propagation, and provides a simple and actionable default strategy: preserve the original user question until the end of the pipeline.

</details>


### [159] [Beyond Surface Forms: A Comprehensive, Mechanism-Oriented Taxonomy of Indirect Linguistic Encoding for LLM-Based Coded Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27314)
*Hamid Reza Firoozfar,Mohammadsadegh Abolhasani,Reza Mousavi,Paul Jen-Hwa Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机制的间接语言表达（ILE）分类体系，不依赖于交际意图，而是根据编码与解码的底层操作对ILE进行分类。通过在TikTok和Bluesky的2000条标注帖子上结合LLM提示进行评测，该分类体系在文档级和片段级检测任务上均优于四种现有分类体系和无数分类基线，准确率提升4.7%，F1提升5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体用户为规避审查和监控，大量使用间接语言表达（如算法黑话、委婉语、对抗性混淆）来隐藏敏感含义。现有分类体系多按交际意图或使用场景划分，缺乏对底层编码机制的关注，限制了检测系统的泛化能力。作者试图建立一种机制导向的、统一的ILE分类体系，作为检测新兴编码语言的稳定框架。

Method: 1. 梳理现有ILE相关文献，归纳出五类编码机制：语音变换、字形变换、语义替换、句法重组、语境依赖；2. 构建面向机制的ILE分类体系，独立于交际目标；3. 将分类体系嵌入LLM提示，在TikTok和Bluesky的2000条人工标注帖子上进行文档级和片段级检测评测；4. 与四种现有分类体系（如按意图、领域等划分）及无数分类基线对比，使用三种LLM评估准确率和F1。

Result: 所提分类体系在三种LLM上均取得最优的文档级和片段级检测性能，准确率比最优基线提升4.7%，F1提升5.4%。结果验证了机制导向、综合性的分类体系作为编码语言检测的稳定支架的有效性。

Conclusion: 一个全面且面向机制的分类体系能够为检测不断涌现的间接语言表达提供稳健的结构化支撑，比基于意图或场景的分类更具泛化能力，可作为内容审核系统的有效输入。该研究为算法话语分析与自动化内容审核提供了新的方法论基础。

Abstract: To avoid moderation and surveillance on social media, some users routinely invent indirect linguistic expressions (ILE) that camouflage sensitive meanings. Such expressions surface as algospeak, euphemisms, and adversarial obfuscation, depending on intent and context, and they involve recurring encoding mechanisms. We propose a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy of ILE that abstracts away from communicative goals and instead categorizes the underlying operations through which meaning is encoded and recovered. We evaluate the taxonomy by incorporating it into LLM prompts and comparing it with four existing taxonomies and a no-taxonomy baseline, using 2,000 manually annotated TikTok and Bluesky posts. The proposed taxonomy attains the strongest document- and span-level performance across the three LLMs, achieving an improvement of 4.7% in accuracy and 5.4% in F1 over the best-performing benchmark. The empirical results reveal the importance of a comprehensive, mechanism-oriented taxonomy as a stable scaffold for detecting emerging coded language and a useful input to content moderation. Disclaimer: This paper contains content that may be profane, vulgar, or offensive.

</details>


### [160] [LLM-Based Examination of Eligibility Criteria from Securities Prospectuses at the German Central Bank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27316)
*Serhii Hamotskyi,Akash Kumar Gautam,Christian Hänig*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将大语言模型（LLM）应用于德国央行的证券抵押品资格审查任务，提出一种生成式信息抽取流水线（提取-标准化-解释），并引入基于LLM-as-a-judge的语义评估方法，在文档级资格审查中实现高达91%的精确率，同时保持保守的误接受控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 德国央行需手动从冗长、半结构化、双语（德英）的招股说明书中验证证券抵押品资格，传统NER方法受限于OCR噪声、语言变体、刚性跨度约束以及高昂的人工标注成本，亟需更灵活高效的自动化方案。

Method: 采用大语言模型构建生成式信息抽取流水线，将任务分解为三个阶段：（1）关键信息提取；（2）数据标准化；（3）资格解释与判定。同时提出LLM-as-a-judge的基于语义的评估方法，替代传统基于位置的评估指标。

Result: LLM系统在文档级资格审查中精确率达到91%，展现出保守的操作特性（低误接受率），基于语义的评估方法相比位置指标更能反映任务真实需求。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的方法可有效处理噪声OCR文本和混合语言场景，生成式IE范式比传统NER更适合该类复杂文档审核任务，且语义评估方法为该领域提供了更合理的评测标准。

Abstract: Verifying the eligibility of securities as collateral is a key responsibility of the German Central Bank. However, manually verifying these assets against legal and financial criteria within lengthy, semi-structured, and often bilingual prospectuses is a resource-intensive task. While previous efforts utilized traditional Named Entity Recognition (NER) for information extraction, these methods can struggle with OCR noise, linguistic variance, and rigid span-based constraints, and the need for manually annotated training data for each relevant annotation type. In this paper, we present the first case study applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to the eligibility examination process, shifting the paradigm toward a generative Information Extraction pipeline. Our approach decomposes the task into extraction, normalization, and interpretation, allowing for greater flexibility in handling noisy text and interleaved German-English content. We further introduce a value-based evaluation methodology using LLM-as-a-judge, which offers a more semantic assessment than location-based metrics. Our results demonstrate that LLM-based systems achieve high precision (up to 91%) in document-level eligibility, exhibiting a conservative operating profile that minimizes false acceptance.

</details>


### [161] [Empowering GUI Agents via Autonomous Experience Exploration and Hindsight Experience Utilization for Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27330)
*Tianyi Men,Zhuoran Jin,Pengfei Cao,Yubo Chen,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了PEEU方法，通过自主探索环境发现经验并利用后见经验合成高层次训练数据，以提升小型多模态大语言模型在GUI任务规划中的跨网站泛化能力；同时提出TDHAF框架分析组合泛化行为，实验表明7B模型可超越32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开源多模态大语言模型（MLLM）虽然成本低、隐私保护好，但在网页GUI任务规划中存在规划能力弱、跨网站泛化能力差的问题，需要一种有效的方法来提升其任务分解和规划能力。

Method: 提出PEEU（Planning Experience Exploration and Utilization）方法：自主探索环境以发现经验，利用后见经验（hindsight experience）合成严格对齐的高层训练数据。同时提出TDHAF（Task Decomposition Hierarchical Analysis Framework）框架，从低、中、高三个粒度层级系统研究组合泛化行为。

Result: 实验表明，PEEU训练的7B模型在真实世界基准上达到30.6%准确率，超越了更大的Qwen2.5-VL-32B模型。分析揭示：掌握低层原子技能并不能保证高层规划能力，而高层任务训练能带来更强的分布外（OOD）泛化能力。

Conclusion: 构建后见高层任务并利用经验对小型MLLM的分布外规划能力至关重要，PEEU方法为高效、隐私友好的GUI智能体提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Multimodal web agents can assist humans in operating repetitive GUI tasks, where effective task planning is essential for decomposing complex tasks into executable actions. While small open source MLLMs are cost efficient and privacy preserving compared with commercial large models, they suffer from weak planning and limited cross website generalization. To address these limitations, we introduce the planning experience exploration and utilization (PEEU) method, which autonomously explores environments to discover experiences and utilizes hindsight experience to synthesize strictly aligned, high level training data. To quantitatively analyze the generalization behaviors driving this performance, we propose the task decomposition hierarchical analysis framework (TDHAF) to systematically study compositional generalization across three task granularities: low, middle and high levels. Our analysis reveals that mastering low level atomic skills does not guarantee high level planning competence, while high level task training yields stronger OOD generalization. Experiments on real world benchmarks demonstrate PEEU's superior effectiveness: our 7B model achieves 30.6% accuracy, outperforming the much larger Qwen2.5-VL-32B model. These demonstrate constructing hindsight high level tasks and leveraging experiences is crucial for OOD planning abilities of small MLLMs.

</details>


### [162] [Mapping Political-Elite Networks in Europe with a Multilingual Joint Entity-Relation Extraction Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27347)
*Kirill Solovev,Jana Lasser*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化、完全开源的跨语言实体关系联合抽取流水线，能够从海量非结构化新闻语料中构建带符号的时序知识图谱。该流水线结合了基于Span的NER、Wikidata实体链接的三阶段级联，以及基于本体约束的混合专家模型进行有向符号关系抽取。在奥地利和波兰的案例研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治精英是形成寻租联盟还是构建公民网络，是比较政治学的核心问题。然而，大规模观察这类复杂、非正式、对抗性的关系历来依赖密集的人工编码，而自动化文本分析方法多局限于简单的共现分析。现有LLM方法虽提供了新路径，但存在依赖闭源API、缺乏跨语言能力、实体解析扩展性差等局限。

Method: 提出一个模块化全开源流水线：(1) 基于Span的命名实体识别(NER)；(2) 三阶段链接级联，将mention映射到跨语言的Wikidata标识符；(3) 高吞吐量、本体约束的混合专家(Mixture-of-Experts)模型，通过引导解码抽取有向的、带符号的关系，并锚定于领域本体。以3491条关系的金标准进行全覆盖抽检验证。

Result: 在金标准测试中，严格匹配正确率达68.2%，宽松匹配达93.7%。奥地利案例中，重建了一个政党从成立到分裂、人员流向后续派系及刑事定罪的完整生命周期；波兰案例中，揭示了国有企业庇护的重叠经济与治理网络，以及公民平台(PO)与法律与公正党(PiS)两党对立中结构均衡的带符号冲突网络。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了原始多语言文本与结构化关系数据之间的鸿沟，为跨国实证计算社会科学提供了稳健、可复现的基础。

Abstract: Whether political elites organise into rent-seeking coalitions that capture public resources or civic networks that sustain governance is a central question in comparative politics. Yet observing these complex, informal, and adversarial ties at scale has historically required intensive manual coding, while automated text-as-data methods have largely been limited to simple co-occurrence. Recent large language model (LLM) approaches offer a path forward but often rely on proprietary APIs, lack cross-lingual capability, and struggle with scalable entity resolution. We present a modular, fully open-weight pipeline for multilingual joint entity-relation extraction that builds signed, temporal knowledge graphs from massive unstructured news corpora. It combines span-based named-entity recognition (NER) with a three-stage linking cascade mapping mentions to language-independent Wikidata identifiers; a high-throughput, ontology-constrained mixture-of-experts model then uses guided decoding to extract directed, signed relationships grounded in a domain ontology. A full-coverage spot-check against a 3491-relation gold standard shows high textual correctness (68.2% strict to 93.7% lenient). Two large-scale case studies validate the pipeline against the public record. In Austria, it reconstructs a political party's complete lifecycle, dating internal fractures and tracking personnel into successor factions and court convictions. In a Polish corpus, it uncovers the overlapping economic and governance networks of state-enterprise patronage, alongside the structurally balanced, signed conflict network of the polarized Civic Platform (Platforma Obywatelska, PO)--Law and Justice (Prawo i Sprawiedliwość, PiS) duopoly. By bridging raw multilingual text and structured relational data, our framework provides a robust, replicable foundation for cross-national empirical computational social science.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [Explainable Outlier Detection for Interval-valued Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26307)
*Catarina P. Loureiro,M. Rosário Oliveira,Paula Brito,Lina Oliveira*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值的区间值数据异常值可解释性方法，在区间最小协方差行列式（IMCD）估计器框架下，推导出鲁棒区间马氏距离平方的Shapley值闭合表达式，实现对异常值的细粒度分解（中心、范围及交叉项贡献），并扩展至Shapley交互指数以捕捉变量间 pairwise 交互效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性在异常检测中日益重要，但对于区间值数据这类复杂数据结构，异常值的可解释性研究几乎空白。现有方法难以解释区间值观测为何被判定为异常，也缺乏对变量层面贡献的分解能力。

Method: 基于区间最小协方差行列式（IMCD）估计器的异常检测框架，引入Shapley值概念来解释区间值观测的异常性。推导了鲁棒区间马氏距离平方的Shapley值闭合表达式，实现变量贡献的高效计算。分解涵盖中心、范围及交叉项三部分。进一步扩展Shapley交互指数以捕捉驱动异常行为的变量 pairwise 交互。

Result: Shapley值闭合表达式使变量贡献计算高效可行；细粒度分解能够区分异常来源于中心偏移、范围异常还是交叉效应；Shapley值可辅助识别在多元层面不显著但在单变量层面存在的胞元异常（cellwise outliers）；Shapley交互指数能揭示变量间的 pairwise 交互对异常行为的驱动作用。在两个真实数据集上验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法为区间值数据的异常检测提供了可解释性框架，实现了对异常来源的细粒度归因，兼具理论闭合解的计算效率与实用价值，在识别胞元异常和变量交互方面具有独特优势。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly recognized as a key aspect of outlier detection. However, for complex data structures such as interval-valued data, it remains largely unexplored. Building on an outlier detection framework based on the Interval Minimum Covariance Determinant estimator, we propose a novel approach to explain the outlyingness of interval-valued observations using the concept of the Shapley value. We derive a closed-form expression for the Shapley value of the squared robust Interval-Mahalanobis distance, enabling efficient computation of variable contributions. This formulation allows for a fine-grained interpretation of outliers, providing a detailed decomposition into contributions from centers, ranges, and cross-terms of the interval-valued observations. Moreover, the Shapley value is closely connected to the concept of cellwise outliers, as it can help identify variable-specific outliers that may not be evident at multivariate level. We further extend the framework through the Shapley interaction index to capture pairwise variable interactions driving atypical behavior. The practical utility of the proposed approach is illustrated through two real-world datasets.

</details>


### [164] [Variance Deltas for Visualizing and Explaining Posterior Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26309)
*Collin Cademartori*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"方差增量"(variance deltas)的交互式可视化系统，用于从贝叶斯后验分布中发现未观测信息——这些信息若被观测到，将显著减少对目标量的不确定性。系统将缺失信息组织为树状结构，支持自动候选子集构建和交互式操作，并在因果推断和选举预测两个场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在观测性研究中，数据生成过程和样本量不可控，无法先验地保证估计精度。但即便数据不足以确定目标量，它们仍可能揭示"缺失什么"——即哪些未观测信息一旦获得就能显著降低不确定性。现有方法缺乏系统性地从后验分布中交互式发现此类缺失信息的工具。

Method: 提出variance deltas交互式可视化系统：(1)将缺失信息表示为未观测模型量的子集；(2)按各子集对目标量不确定性的解释程度，将子集组织成树状结构；(3)基于最小用户输入自动构建候选子集；(4)实现子集分割与合并的交互操作，支持高效发现有用解释。以软件包形式提供。

Result: 在两个场景中验证：(1)模拟因果推断问题中，发现治疗效应参数的非直观不确定性解释；(2)基于真实民调数据、含多种偏差源的预测模型中，发现总体比例的非直观不确定性解释。

Conclusion: variance deltas系统能够有效帮助研究者在贝叶斯框架下交互式地探索和发现哪些未观测信息是导致后验不确定性的关键因素，即使这些解释并非直观可得。该方法增强了观测性研究中不确定性来源的可解释性。

Abstract: In observational settings, where the data generating process and possibly the sample size are not controlled, it is typically impossible to guarantee a priori that quantities of interest will be estimated with sufficient precision. However, even when the data do not determine the quantities of interest, they may still allow determination of what is missing -- unobserved information which, if observed, would meaningfully reduce uncertainty. We propose an interactive visualization system, termed variance deltas, to enable the discovery of such missing information from a Bayesian posterior distribution. This system, which we provide as a software package, represents missing information as subsets of unobserved model quantities, organized into a tree based on how well each subset explains uncertainty about the quantity of interest. This system both automates the construction of candidate subsets from minimal user input and implements interactive operations for the division and combination of subsets, allowing the efficient discovery of interesting and useful explanations. We demonstrate this system by using it to discover nonobvious explanations of uncertainty for (1) a treatment effect parameter in a simulated causal inference problem and (2) a population proportion in a forecasting model of real polling data with many sources of bias.

</details>


### [165] [A unified approach to outlier identification for mixed-type data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26324)
*Efthymios Costa,Christian Hennig*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对混合类型数据（包含连续变量和有序变量）的异常值识别方法。该方法以多元高斯分布为非异常值的参考分布，对有序变量采用潜在高斯假设，并结合稳健的MCD估计器进行参数估计，扩展后能处理有序变量潜在信息不可观测的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集中常同时包含连续变量和有序分类变量，但现有异常值检测方法多仅适用于单一类型数据。需要一种能有效处理混合类型数据、且对异常值具有稳健性的异常值识别方法。

Method: 以多元高斯分布为非异常值参考分布，对有序变量假设潜在高斯生成机制；采用稳健的最小协方差行列式（MCD）估计器估计非异常值的高斯参数，并将其扩展以应对有序变量潜在高斯信息不可观测的问题；给出了崩溃点定理，证明替换观测值不会阻止极端异常值被识别。

Result: 在多种污染类型的合成数据上，该方法实现了高检测率和低假阳性率；在Airbnb房源数据（包含连续和有序属性）的实际应用中验证了其实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效处理混合连续-有序类型数据的异常值检测，具有良好的统计理论基础和实际应用价值，填补了混合数据类型异常值检测的方法学空白。

Abstract: We present an outlier identification method for mixed type data sets comprising continuous and ordinal variables. We define outliers based on using a multivariate Gaussian distribution as reference distribution for non-outliers, with a latent Gaussian assumed for ordinal variables. The proposed algorithm is based on the robust Minimum Covariance Determinant estimator for estimating the parameters of the multivariate Gaussian for the non-outliers. This is extended to account for the fact that the full Gaussian information underlying the ordinal variables is not observed. A breakdown theorem shows that replacing observations will noty stop extreme enough outliers from being identified. The effectiveness of our approach is demonstrated via simulations on synthetic data with various types of contamination, achieving high detection and low false positive rates. Practical relevance is illustrated through an application to Airbnb listing data containing both continuous and ordinal attributes.

</details>


### [166] [Incomplete Matrix Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26325)
*Khaled Fouda,Aurélie Labbe,Karim Oualkacha*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出不完全矩阵回归（IMR）框架，将行/列协变量信息与低秩矩阵补全相结合，通过Lasso惩罚处理协变量效应、利用岭型惩罚融入已知相似结构，并提供可扩展的交替最小二乘算法，在模拟和实际数据中表现出竞争性预测精度和低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统矩阵补全仅利用观测矩阵的稀疏带噪条目进行低秩恢复，但推荐系统、城市出行等应用中常伴有行/列上的辅助协变量及行/列间的依赖结构，现有方法未能有效整合这些信息。因此需要一种能同时利用协变量信息和已知相似结构的统一矩阵补全框架。

Method: 提出不完全矩阵回归（IMR），将目标矩阵分解为截距项、经Lasso惩罚的协变量效应项、以及捕捉残差低秩结构的潜在因子项；通过岭型惩罚将空间/时间核等已知相似结构融入潜在因子。估计采用模块化交替最小二乘算法，各模型组件可灵活增减。给出了Lasso估计量和矩阵补全估计量的非渐近误差界。

Result: 理论层面建立了与各自文献中标准速率一致的非渐近误差界。模拟研究和两个实际数据应用表明，IMR以远低于更复杂方法的计算成本，取得了具有竞争力的预测精度。方法已实现为R包IMR。

Conclusion: IMR框架有效整合协变量信息与低秩结构，在统计精度和计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，为含辅助信息的矩阵补全问题提供了一种灵活、可扩展且理论有保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Matrix completion seeks to recover a low-rank matrix from a sparse and noisy subset of its entries. In many applications, such as recommendation systems and urban mobility, the observed matrix is accompanied by auxiliary covariates on its rows and columns and exhibits dependence across them. We propose Incomplete Matrix Regression (IMR), a distribution-free penalized regression framework that integrates such information into matrix completion. The target matrix is modeled as the sum of intercepts, covariate effects regularized by a Lasso penalty, and a low-rank latent component that captures structure unexplained by the covariates. Known similarity structures, such as spatial and temporal kernels, are incorporated through ridge-type penalties on the latent factors. For estimation, we provide a scalable alternating least-squares algorithm whose modular form allows us to include or exclude individual model components without rederiving the updates. We establish non-asymptotic error bounds for both the Lasso and matrix completion estimators that are consistent with standard rates in their respective literature. Through simulation studies and two real-data applications, we demonstrate that the proposed method attains predictive accuracy competitive with more complex methods at a small fraction of their computational cost. The methodology is implemented in the R package IMR.

</details>


### [167] [Robust Estimation of Polychoric Correlation for Complex Survey Designs Using Minimum Divergence Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26351)
*Siqi Wei,David Kepplinger,Anand N. Vidyashankar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对调查数据中的污染问题，本文提出了基于Hellinger距离(HD)和负指数差异(NED)两种最小差异准则的稳健多分格相关系数估计方法，并通过Horvitz-Thompson调整纳入复杂抽样权重。HD方法引入岭回归和Lasso惩罚变体，具有一致性和渐近正态性；模拟表明，惩罚HD方法在一致性污染下MSE最低，NED在不一致性污染及复合误设下表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准多分格相关系数的极大似然估计对调查数据中的污染（如回答误差、访员效应、粗心作答）高度敏感，且对所有观测赋予相等权重，忽略数据质量差异。需要开发在复杂调查设计下具有稳健性的估计方法。

Method: 基于Hellinger距离(HD)和负指数差异(NED)两种最小差异准则构建稳健估计量；通过Horvitz-Thompson调整的单元格频率纳入调查权重。HD估计量进一步引入岭回归(Ridge)和Lasso惩罚变体，对冗余参数进行正则化而保持相关系数不受惩罚。推导了一致性和渐近正态性，并给出反映抽样设计的 sandwich 协方差。分析了影响函数的有界性特征。

Result: 影响函数有限但非一致有界，反映Hellinger距离对稀疏单元格的敏感性。模拟采用泊松比例规模抽样，考察三种污染几何形态（一致性上尾、一致性下尾、不一致混合角点）与标准/非标准潜在边际分布的组合。结果表明：惩罚HD方法在一致性污染下达到最低MSE；NED在不一致性污染及"污染-复合误设"复合效应下表现最佳。

Conclusion: 两类估计量具有互补优势，研究人员可根据调查实践中预期的污染模式选择合适方法：预期一致性污染时优先选用惩罚HD方法，预期不一致性污染或复合误设时优先选用NED方法。

Abstract: Standard maximum likelihood estimation of polychoric correlations is highly sensitive to contamination in survey data, including response errors, interviewer effects, and careless responding, yet assigns equal weight to all observations regardless of data quality. We develop robust estimators for polychoric correlation under complex survey designs based on two minimum divergence criteria -- Hellinger distance (HD) and negative exponential disparity (NED) -- incorporating survey weights through Horvitz--Thompson adjusted cell frequencies. For HD, we propose penalized Ridge and Lasso variants that regularize nuisance parameters while leaving the correlation unpenalized, and establish consistency and asymptotic normality with a sandwich covariance reflecting the sampling design. The influence function is finite but not uniformly bounded, reflecting Hellinger's sensitivity to sparse cells. Simulations under Poisson proportional-to-size sampling examine three contamination geometries -- concordant upper, concordant lower, and discordant mixed corner -- crossed with standard and non-standard latent marginals. The two estimator classes offer complementary advantages: penalized HD methods achieve the lowest mean squared error under concordant contamination, while NED performs best under discordant contamination and under compound misspecification--contamination effects. We provide practical guidelines for method selection based on anticipated contamination patterns in survey practice.

</details>


### [168] [Learning rate selection via weighted Fisher divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26478)
*Takahiro Onizuka*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于加权Fisher散度的学习率选择方法，用于校准广义贝叶斯框架下的Gibbs后验分布，解决了模型误设情况下可信集缺乏正确不确定性解释的校准问题，所选学习率小于等于Fisher信息匹配学习率，并通过数值实验和真实数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义贝叶斯推断通过引入基于损失的似然提供了灵活的建模框架，但其灵活性带来了校准问题：当模型误设时，传统贝叶斯信息等式失效，未校准的Gibbs后验产生的可信集无法提供正确的不确定性量化。因此需要一种系统的方法来选择学习率以校准广义后验。

Method: 作者引入加权Fisher散度（weighted Fisher divergence），衡量广义后验的渐近分布与具有夹层型方差（sandwich-type variance）的正态分布之间的差异。通过最小化该散度，推导出学习率的闭式解。该解将Fisher信息匹配学习率作为特例包含在内，并在重要特殊情形下不大于后者。

Result: 所选学习率具有闭式表达式，包含Fisher信息匹配学习率作为特例；在重要的特殊情形下，所选学习率不超过Fisher信息匹配学习率。数值示例和真实数据分析表明该方法有效。

Conclusion: 提出的加权Fisher散度方法为广义贝叶斯框架中的学习率选择提供了理论上有依据、计算上可行的校准方案，有效解决了模型误设下的不确定性量化问题。

Abstract: The general Bayesian approach provides a flexible modeling framework by introducing a loss-based likelihood. A general posterior has a learning rate, which controls the relative weight of the loss-based likelihood and the prior. The flexibility of general Bayesian inference comes with an important calibration problem, especially under model misspecification. In such cases, the conventional Bayesian information identity fails, and credible sets derived from an uncalibrated Gibbs posterior need not have the desired uncertainty interpretation. This paper aims to select the learning rate used to calibrate a general posterior. By introducing the weighted Fisher divergence between the asymptotic distribution of the general posterior and a normal distribution with sandwich-type variance, we provide a closed-form expression for the selected learning rate. The selected learning rate includes the Fisher information matching learning rate as a special case and is no larger than it in an important special case. Numerical examples and a real data analysis demonstrate the usefulness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [169] [$λ$-PSD: Scalable Approximate SNR-Optimised Polynomial Stein Discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26621)
*Minh-Long Nguyen,Thanh-Long Vu,Christopher Drovandi,Leah F. South,Trung-Tin Nguyen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 该文指出多项式Stein差异（PSD）在提高多项式阶数时信噪比（SNR²）可能指数衰减，为此提出λ-PSD，通过将Stein差异构造重铸为SNR²最大化问题，在低维子空间中实现协方差感知的加权，从而避免SNR²崩溃并显著提升检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多项式Stein差异（PSD）虽计算可扩展，但其统计特性不明。作者发现增大多项式阶数并不直接优化信噪比，反而可能导致SNR²指数级崩溃，因此需要一种SNR感知的Stein差异构造方法。

Method: 1. 分析PSD的SNR²行为，揭示其随多项式阶数指数衰减的失效模式；2. 将Stein差异构造形式化为显式的SNR²最大化问题，转化为Stein特征上的Rayleigh商；3. 提出λ-PSD：在低维子空间中定义协方差感知的近似可扩展重加权方案。

Result: 理论上，在高斯设定下λ-PSD避免了SNR²指数崩溃，实现稳定SNR²；实证上，λ-PSD在保持样本数线性时间复杂度的同时显著提升了检验功效。

Conclusion: SNR感知设计对可扩展Stein差异至关重要，λ-PSD在可扩展性与统计功效之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Polynomial Stein discrepancies (PSD) provide a scalable alternative to kernel Stein methods for measuring sample quality and goodness-of-fit testing, but their statistical properties remain poorly understood. We show that increasing polynomial degree primarily amplifies signal without adequately controlling variance, rather than directly optimising the signal-to-noise ratio (SNR). Under suitable assumptions, this might lead to a failure mode in which the $\text{SNR}^2$ can provably decay exponentially with polynomial degree. Motivated by this observation, we reformulate Stein discrepancy construction as an explicit $\text{SNR}^2$ maximisation problem, yielding a Rayleigh quotient over Stein features. This perspective motivates $λ$-PSD, an approximate scalable covariance-aware reweighting scheme defined in a low-dimensional subspace. Under Gaussian settings, we show that $λ$-PSD avoids the exponential $\text{SNR}^2$ collapse and achieves a stable $\text{SNR}^2$. Empirically, $λ$-PSD substantially improves test power while retaining linear-time complexity in the number of samples, highlighting the importance of SNR-aware design for scalable Stein discrepancies.

</details>


### [170] [A Unified Three-Stage Weighting Framework for Causal Inference and Mediation Analysis under Case-Control Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26543)
*Tarikul Islam,Mahbub A. H. M. Latif*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的三阶段加权(3S-weighting)框架，用于从病例-对照研究中估计因果总效应和路径特异性因果中介效应。该方法利用密度比学习和标签偏移校正估计未知的群体结局患病率，然后构建基于患病率的设计权重来重建目标群体分布，最后在边际结构模型框架内应用稳定的因果和中介权重，以一致地恢复群体因果参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病例-对照研究因结局依赖的回顾性抽样会扭曲群体结局分布，给因果推断带来根本性挑战。传统分析忽略回顾性抽样可导致总效应和中介效应的严重偏倚，需要一种能从回顾性样本中恢复目标群体因果参数的统一方法。

Method: 提出三阶段加权(3S-weighting)框架：第一阶段利用密度比学习和标签偏移校正，结合外部协变量信息估计未知群体结局患病率；第二阶段使用基于患病率的设计权重从回顾性样本重建目标群体分布；第三阶段在边际结构模型框架内应用稳定的因果权重和中介权重，估计总效应及路径特异性效应（纯直接效应、纯间接效应和交互效应）。

Result: 模拟研究表明，忽略回顾性抽样的传统分析在总效应和中介效应估计中均产生显著偏倚；而所提方法在不同抽样场景下均能一致地恢复目标群体因果参数。基于NHANES数据的应用进一步展示了该框架的实际可操作性和效用。

Conclusion: 3S-weighting框架为病例-对照研究中的因果推断和因果中介分析提供了统一且有效的解决方案，能够纠正回顾性抽样带来的偏倚，在理论和应用层面均有重要价值。

Abstract: Case-control studies are widely used in epidemiology and biomedical research because they provide substantial efficiency gains when outcomes are rare or prospective follow-up is impractical. However, retrospective outcome-dependent sampling distorts the population outcome distribution, creating fundamental challenges for causal inference. We propose a unified three-stage weighting (3S-weighting) framework for causal inference and causal mediation analysis from case--control studies. The proposed approach first estimates the unknown population outcome prevalence using density-ratio learning and label-shift correction combined with externally available covariate information. Next, prevalence-based design weights are used to reconstruct the target population distribution from the retrospective sample. Finally, stabilized causal and mediation weights are applied within a marginal structural modeling framework to estimate total and pathway-specific causal effects, including the pure direct effect, pure indirect effect, and interaction effect. Simulation studies demonstrate that conventional analyses that ignore retrospective sampling can produce substantial bias in both total and mediation effect estimates, whereas the proposed approach consistently recovers the target population causal parameters across a range of sampling scenarios. An application of data from the National Health and Nutrition Examination Survey further illustrates the practical implementation and utility of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [171] [Fast Robust Regression via Orthogonal Block Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26611)
*Anthony Christidis,Matias Salibian-Barrera*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维数据下鲁棒回归MM估计量的初始化难题，提出ROBU算法：利用分块坐标下降思想，在低维变量块上寻找干净子样本作为初始点，大幅降低所需子样本数量，实现可扩展的鲁棒回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于随机子集的鲁棒回归初始化方法所需子样本数随预测变量数指数增长，无法扩展到中高维模型；近期非随机策略在大样本、多参数时同样扩展性差。需要一个兼具鲁棒性与计算可扩展性的初始估计方法。

Method: 提出ROBU（Robust Orthogonal Block Updates）算法：将解释变量划分为低维正交块，对每个块分别用少量随机子样本拟合MM估计量获得干净初始点，再通过块坐标下降迭代更新各块系数，最终结合所有块得到全局鲁棒回归估计。

Result: 大量仿真与蛋白基因组学实际数据应用表明，ROBU估计量具有良好的鲁棒性，且与现有算法相比表现更优，能在高维场景下高效计算。

Conclusion: ROBU通过分块策略成功解决了鲁棒回归MM估计量在高维数据下的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模数据集的稳健分析提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Robust regression methods, particularly MM-estimators, are essential for analyzing datasets where heavy-tailed noise or high-leverage outliers may be present. Algorithms to compute these estimators are iterative and rely on having a good initial point. A widely-used probabilistic approach to obtaining an initial regression estimator that is not affected by outliers consists of fitting linear regression models to many random subsets of the training data. Unfortunately, the number of subsets that need to be considered in order to have a high probability of finding one that is clean of outliers grows exponentially with the number of predictors. This renders the approach unfeasible for models with a moderate to large numbers of variables. Alternative non-stochastic strategies that have been proposed recently also fail to scale well when the number of parameters and observations are large. To overcome this problem, we propose a highly scalable algorithm based on block-coordinate descent. Our Robust Orthogonal Block Updates (ROBU) algorithm uses lower-dimensional blocks of explanatory variables, which require much fewer sub-samples to find a good initial point with high probability. Extensive simulations and a proteogenomics data application illustrate the robustness properties of the estimators computed with ROBU and demonstrate that they compare favourably to those calculated with existing algorithms.

</details>


### [172] [Doubly Robust Quadratic Inference Functions for Causal Inference in Cluster Randomized Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26630)
*Hengshi Yu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双重稳健的二次推断函数（DR-QIF）估计量，将双重稳健伪结果与QIF扩展得分方程结合，用于处理集群随机试验中的协变量不平衡问题。当倾向得分模型或结果回归模型之一正确设定时，DR-QIF一致估计平均处理效应，且在工作相关结构误设时比DR-GEE更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的QIF方法未考虑由于协变量不平衡导致的混杂问题，这在观察性集群随机试验或需要预后协变量调整的CRT中普遍存在。需要一种既能处理相关数据、又具有双重稳健性且效率更高的方法。

Method: 提出DR-QIF估计量：首先基于倾向得分模型和结果回归模型构建双重稳健伪结果（pseudo-outcomes），然后将这些伪结果代入QIF的扩展得分方程中进行估计。该方法在横向CRT中与DR-GEE代数等价，但在纵向CRT中展现出更高的渐近效率。

Result: DR-QIF在任一模型正确设定时均一致；工作相关结构误设时，DR-QIF比DR-GEE更有效。效率增益在纵向CRT中显著，在N=120、T=8次重复测量时达到3.5%。蒙特卡洛模拟验证了有限样本性质，并利用WASH Benefits Kenya CRT数据进行了实证说明。

Conclusion: DR-QIF为集群随机试验中的边际回归提供了一种兼具双重稳健性和更高效率的推断方法，特别适用于存在强时间相关性的纵向CRT设计，相比现有DR-GEE方法具有明显的效率优势。

Abstract: Quadratic inference functions (QIF) provide a robust and efficient alternative to generalized estimating equations (GEE) for marginal regression with correlated data, particularly in cluster randomized trials (CRTs). However, existing QIF methodology does not account for confounding due to covariate imbalance between treatment arms, a common concern in observational CRTs or CRTs with prognostic covariate adjustment. We propose a doubly robust QIF (DR-QIF) estimator that combines doubly robust pseudo-outcomes, constructed from propensity score and outcome regression models, with the QIF extended score equations. The DR-QIF estimator is consistent for the average treatment effect when either the propensity score model or the outcome regression model is correctly specified, but not necessarily both. We show that DR-QIF is more efficient than doubly robust GEE (DR-GEE) when the working correlation structure is misspecified, and we characterize the asymptotic efficiency gain analytically. For cross-sectional CRTs the two estimators are algebraically identical; efficiency gains emerge in longitudinal CRTs with strong temporal correlation, reaching 3.5% at N=120 and T=8 repeated measures. Finite-sample properties are evaluated via Monte Carlo simulation, and the method is illustrated using data from the WASH Benefits Kenya cluster randomized trial.

</details>


### [173] [Multivariable Mendelian randomization with weak instruments: a comparison of Bayesian and frequentist methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26638)
*Andrew J. Grant,Ashish Patel,Stephen Burgess*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯框架下的多变量孟德尔随机化方法MVMR-Pony，用于缓解弱工具变量偏倚问题。模拟研究表明，在遗传效应相关、测量误差和中介等导致弱工具偏倚的情境下，MVMR-Pony在偏倚、覆盖率、I类错误率和统计功效方面均优于现有频率学派方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多变量孟德尔随机化中，遗传工具变量可能在边际上与暴露强相关，但在条件于所有其他暴露后变为弱工具变量，这一问题比单变量情形更难识别和解决。现有缓解多变量弱工具偏倚的方法较为有限，亟需新的方法论。

Method: 提出MVMR-Pony方法，基于贝叶斯框架对多变量孟德尔随机化进行建模，通过引入先验分布和 posterior inference 来缓解弱工具变量偏倚。通过模拟研究将其与现有频率学派方法进行系统比较。

Result: 模拟研究表明，在由遗传效应相关、测量误差和中介引起的弱工具变量偏倚情境下，MVMR-Pony在偏倚、覆盖率、I类错误率和统计功效四项指标上均优于频率学派方法。

Conclusion: MVMR-Pony作为一种贝叶斯方法，能够有效缓解多变量孟德尔随机化中的弱工具变量偏倚，相比现有频率学派方法具有显著优势。

Abstract: Weak instruments are a well known limitation for valid causal inference in Mendelian randomization studies. In the single exposure setting, weak instrument bias can be mitigated by selecting genetic instruments which are strongly associated with the exposure according to p-value and/or F-statistic thresholds. However, in the multi-exposure setting, genetic instruments may be strongly associated with an exposure but weakly associated with it conditional on all other exposures in the analysis. It is therefore more difficult to guarantee conditionally strong instruments in multivariable Mendelian randomization. Weak instrument bias can be mitigated using modelling approaches, however there are fewer methods for doing this in the multivariable case compared with the single exposure case. In this paper, we consider a method for mitigating weak instrument bias in multivariable Mendelian randomization using a Bayesian framework: MVMR-Pony. We compare this method with existing frequentist methods. We show using simulation studies that the MVMR-Pony method outperforms the frequentist approaches with respect to bias, coverage, type I error rates, and power, across settings where weak instrument bias arises due to correlated genetic effects, measurement error, and mediation.

</details>


### [174] [End-to-end probabilistic hierarchical forecasting of large hierarchies via probabilistic top-down](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26774)
*Lorenzo Zambon,Dario Azzimonti,Giorgio Corani*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: e2eTD是一种快速可扩展的概率预测方法，仅对少量聚合序列建模，通过新颖的概率自顶向下采样算法将预测传播至底层，再通过求和获得所有层级的一致预测。在两个大型零售数据集上达到了最低加权分位数损失，在M5不确定性竞赛中可排第11/892。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售和供应链运营需要在不同聚合层级上产生概率性且层次一致的需求预测，但现有方法（先预测后调和、端到端神经模型）计算成本高、假设限制多或需要专用硬件，在包含数千条时间序列的大规模零售场景中难以高效运行。

Method: 提出e2eTD方法：(1) 仅对层次结构中约0.3%的顶层聚合序列进行概率预测（这些序列更平滑、更可预测）；(2) 提出新颖的概率自顶向下采样算法，将历史分解比例建模为联合分布并在样本内估计，将顶层预测样本传播至底层；(3) 对底层联合样本求和，获得所有聚合层级的概率一致性预测。

Result: 在M5和Favorita两个最大公开零售数据集上，e2eTD在所有聚合层级上实现了最低的加权缩放分位数损失（WSPL）；在M5不确定性竞赛中可排名第11/892；在普通笔记本上，M5约5分钟、Favorita约20分钟即可完成。

Conclusion: e2eTD以极低的计算成本实现了大规模层次时间序列的概率一致性预测，在预测精度和计算效率上均优于现有方法，适合实际零售运营部署。

Abstract: Retail and supply chain operations rely on demand forecasts to drive decisions, from replenishment at the product level to capacity planning at the store level. These forecasts should be probabilistic, to allow risk-aware decisions, and coherent across the aggregation hierarchy, so that decisions taken at different levels are not based on conflicting demand forecasts. However, producing coherent probabilistic forecasts is computationally demanding; at retail scale, with hierarchies of thousands of time series, this cost becomes a first-order operational concern. Existing two-step forecast-then-reconcile procedures and end-to-end neural models scale poorly, rely on restrictive assumptions, or require specialized hardware and engineering effort. We propose e2eTD, a fast and scalable method for probabilistic coherent forecasting of large hierarchical and grouped time series. e2eTD directly forecasts only a small subset of aggregate series (about 0.3\% of the hierarchy in our experiments), which are smoother and thus more predictable than the intermittent bottom series.
