The resulting forecast samples are propagated to the bottom level through a novel probabilistic top-down sampling algorithm, in which the historical disaggregation proportions are modeled as joint distributions, estimated in-sample. Coherent forecasts for all aggregation levels are then obtained by summing the joint bottom-level samples. On the two largest publicly available retail datasets, M5 and Favorita, e2eTD achieves the lowest weighted scaled pinball loss (the M5 competition's probabilistic score) across aggregation levels among all competing methods; it would have ranked 11th of 892 teams in the M5 Uncertainty competition. On a standard laptop, e2eTD runs in about five minutes on M5 ($\sim$40K series) and twenty minutes on Favorita ($\sim$300K series).

</details>


### [175] [Multiple testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26781)
*Jesse Hemerik*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文系统介绍了多重假设检验的基本概念，涵盖多种错误控制准则（如FWER、FDR等）和相应的检验方法，并提供了相关R软件包的参考，可作为研究生层次的教学讲义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维数据分析中，同时进行大量假设检验会导致多重性问题，若不加控制会使得假阳性率急剧膨胀。该讲义旨在为博士生提供统一、系统的多重检验理论框架和实践工具。

Method: 采用综述性、教学性的组织方式，逐一介绍多重检验中的各类错误准则（族系错误率FWER、错误发现率FDR等）以及对应的控制过程（如Bonferroni校正、Holm方法、Benjamini-Hochberg方法等），并辅以R语言实现参考。

Result: 形成了一套完整的多重假设检验教学讲义，清晰界定了不同错误控制准则的定义、适用场景及相互关系，并汇总了实施这些方法的常用R包。早期版本已成功用于博士课程教学。

Conclusion: 该讲义为多重假设检验提供了系统、实用的入门指南，有效连接了理论与应用，适合作为统计与数据科学领域研究生课程的教材或自学参考。

Abstract: This text provides an introduction to multiple hypothesis testing. It covers various error criteria and testing procedures, and includes references to relevant R packages. An earlier version of this text served as the lecture notes for a PhD-level course on multiple testing.

</details>


### [176] [Structured Secant Methods to Select Smoothing Parameters For General Smooth Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26804)
*Joshua Krause,Jelmer P. Borst,Jacolien van Rij*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出qEFS方法——一种准牛顿变体的扩展Fellner-Schall优化方法，通过结构化有限记忆割线近似替代对数似然的Hessian矩阵，将原本需要四阶导数的精确优化降为基本一阶方法，大幅降低了实现难度和计算成本，同时在某些条件下收敛于EFS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义平滑模型中的平滑参数估计需要优化拉普拉斯近似贝叶斯边际似然。现有二阶方法需要Hessian矩阵，精确优化则需四阶导数，推导困难且计算昂贵。因此需要一种更易实现、计算更高效的替代方法。

Method: 提出qEFS（quasi-Newton Extended Fellner-Schall）方法：利用结构化有限记忆割线近似替代对数似然的Hessian，本质上为一阶方法；可对Hessian的子块累积近似，同时约束其余列与真实Hessian匹配，以提升子块近似精度。

Result: 理论证明qEFS在某些条件下收敛于EFS方法，且超越这些条件时仍能提供良好估计；仿真研究验证了其有效性；涉及Hessian的次要任务（置信区间覆盖与模型选择）需要部分近似才能达到接近标称性能；在隐马尔可夫模型和Tweedie模型上展示了实现简便性。

Conclusion: qEFS方法在保持估计质量的同时，显著降低了广义平滑模型中平滑参数优化的实现难度和计算开销，为难以推导高阶导数的复杂模型提供了实用的替代方案。

Abstract: General smooth models replace parameters of a regular likelihood with additive models. The models can include parametric terms, Gaussian random effects, and smooth functions of covariates. The latter are parameterized via a reduced-rank spline basis and regularized via weighted quadratic penalties placed on the basis coefficients. Estimates for these weights (i.e., smoothing parameters) can be obtained by optimizing the Laplace-approximate Bayesian marginal likelihood. Existing (second-order) methods require the Hessian of the log-likelihood to solve this optimization problem approximately - exact optimization requires up to fourth order derivatives - which can be difficult to derive and expensive to evaluate. To address these problems, we present a quasi-Newton variant of the second-order Extended Fellner-Schall (EFS) optimization method. Our qEFS method relies on structured limited-memory secant approximations to the Hessian of the log-likelihood and is principally first-order. However, the approximation can also be accumulated for a sub-block of the Hessian, with the remaining columns being constrained to match those of the actual Hessian. The exact columns then provide additional structure for the sub-block approximation, which becomes more accurate as a result. We show that the qEFS method converges to the EFS method under certain conditions and continues to provide good estimates beyond these circumstances, which we illustrate in simulation studies. Secondary tasks involving the Hessian (confidence interval coverage & model selection) require partial approximations to achieve close to nominal performance. We provide Hidden Markov and Tweedie model examples, for which the qEFS method is substantially easier to implement than alternative methods.

</details>


### [177] [Optimizing Experimental Design for Causal Effect Estimation with Partial Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26818)
*Leopold Mareis*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究在工具变量回归中，如何利用先验信息和预算约束，通过最优分配部分样本（如仅观测 $X_{12}$）与完整样本，最小化因果效应估计的渐近方差，从而在保证统计功效的同时显著降低预算和完整样本需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具变量因果推断中，获取完整样本（$X_1, X_2, X_3$）的成本可能很高。研究者希望利用已有的先验分布信息，在预算约束下智能地决定采集哪些部分样本（如仅采集 $X_{12}$），以最小化估计量的渐近方差，从而以更低成本达到所需的统计精度和功效。

Method: 作者在高斯图模型框架下，推导了不同参数配置下一致估计量的渐近方差表达式。引入每样本成本预算约束，将部分样本与完整样本的最优数量分配建模为一个可解析求解的实数优化问题。基于最优分配方案，进一步给出了检测非零因果效应的显著性水平、统计功效和样本量计算公式。

Result: 该方法能够在特定参数配置下，通过采集部分样本显著降低渐近方差；优化问题在实数域上可解析求解，得出最优的部分样本与完整样本数量；在最优预算分配下，可大幅减少所需预算和完整样本数，同时保持检测非零因果效应的统计功效。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应因果效应估计方法通过最优采样策略，在工具变量回归中实现了成本效益与统计精度的有效平衡。在汽车分析和制药研究等实际应用中展示了方法的优势和适用性。

Abstract: Instrumental variable regression quantifies causal effects between a possibly confounded treatment variable $ X_2 $ and a response variable $ X_3 $ by leveraging an instrument $ X_1 $. Our work considers the setting where some prior information of the joint distribution of $ X_{123} $ is given, potentially through an initial dataset. However, further samples must be gathered to improve the accuracy of the estimation. We show that under specific parameter configurations in a Gaussian graphical model, taking partial samples from, e.g., $ X_{12} $ can reduce the asymptotic variance of a consistent estimator. This idea is developed by adding a budget constraint over the cost per (partial) sample. The optimization problem is analytically solvable over the real numbers and gives the optimal number of requested partial and complete samples. We provide significance level, power, and sample-size calculations for detecting a non-zero causal effect under optimal budget allocation. Our method can considerably reduce the necessary budget and the number of complete samples. Finally, we showcase the advantages and applicability of adaptive causal effect estimation for automotive analytics and pharmaceutical research.

</details>


### [178] [High-dimensional reliability-oriented Shapley effect estimation with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26826)
*Monteiro Lucas,Morio Jérôme,Demange-Chryst Julien,Bachoc François*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于单一失效样本集的可靠性导向Shapley效应估计新方案，利用归一化流估计失效条件下的高维边际密度，并给出相应的误差估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型中存在大量相关输入变量时，传统的可靠性导向Shapley效应估计面临高维条件密度估计困难及计算成本高昂的问题，需要一种仅依赖单一失效样本集的高效估计方法。

Method: 首先将可靠性导向的闭合Sobol指标重新表达为涉及失效条件下边际密度的形式；然后利用归一化流（Normalizing Flows）这一生成式建模工具，基于可用失效样本估计这些可能为高维的边际密度；最后提出一种基于同一失效样本集的误差估计流程。

Result: 在数值算例上验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了方法的若干深刻特性，并为未来研究提供了展望。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在仅有单一失效样本集的情况下，有效处理高维相关输入变量下的可靠性导向Shapley效应估计，归一化流在估计复杂高维条件密度方面表现出色，误差估计流程也为实际应用提供了可靠性保障。

Abstract: This article presents a new estimation scheme for the reliability-oriented Shapley effects when there is a large number of correlated input variables in the model, using a unique sample of failure points. To do so, we first propose a new writing of the reliability-oriented closed Sobol indices involving the marginal densities conditionally to the failure, which may be high-dimensional. Then, we propose to estimate these densities with the available failing samples using Normalizing Flows, powerful tools from generative modeling that enable the estimation of complex high-dimensional densities. In addition, we provide an error estimation procedure relying on the same sample of failing points, which constitutes a new contribution for the estimation of target Shapley effects. Finally, we illustrate our methodology on numerical use-cases, discuss insightful features of our approach and provide prospects for the future.

</details>


### [179] [Semiparametric Inference for Half-Trek Estimators in Linear Structural Equation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26931)
*Leopold Mareis,Nils Sturma,Mathias Drton*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对半路径准则（HTC）识别的线性结构方程模型，作者首次推导了HTC估计量的半参数影响函数及其渐近分布，从而可构造标准误、置信区间和Wald检验，并在Fulton鱼市数据上验证了完整推断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HTC虽为有向混合图中结构系数提供了图形化充分识别条件和闭式估计量，但其渐近分布一直未知，导致无法进行有效统计推断（标准误、置信区间、假设检验）。该文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 作者推导了所有HTC可识别图（含循环图）的HTC估计量的半参数影响函数。该影响函数将目标节点的结构残差与工具变量相结合，并递归校正前期估计阶段引入的不确定性。基于影响函数，证明估计量的渐近正态性，并给出闭式方差公式，从而构造置信域、边际置信区间和针对单个结构系数的Wald检验。

Result: HTC估计量被证明是渐近正态的，其方差可闭式计算。基于此可构建置信区域、边际区间及Wald检验。在Fulton鱼市数据集上，该方法提供了供给对需求因果效应的完整推断摘要。

Conclusion: 该研究弥合了HTC识别理论与实际因果推断之间的鸿沟，使得在含潜在混杂的有向混合图模型中，不仅能够点估计结构系数，还能进行完整的统计推断，显著提升了HTC方法的实用价值。

Abstract: Linear structural equation models on directed mixed graphs encode causal relationships among variables subject to latent confounding. The half-trek criterion (HTC) provides a graphical sufficient condition for the structural coefficients to be rationally identifiable from the observable covariance matrix, and yields a corresponding closed-form rational estimator. Despite this, the asymptotic distribution of the HTC estimator, and hence valid standard errors and confidence regions, have not been derived. We derive the semiparametric influence function of this estimator for all HTC-identified directed mixed graphs, including cyclic ones. The influence function combines the structural residual at the target node with the identification instruments, recursively corrected for uncertainty from earlier estimation stages. The HTC estimator is asymptotically normal with variance computable in closed form, yielding confidence regions, marginal intervals, and Wald tests for individual structural coefficients. Applied to the Fulton Fish Market dataset, our theory delivers a complete inferential summary for the causal effect of supply on demand.

</details>


### [180] [Exact Comparison of Explanatory Strength of Two Dependent Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26949)
*Tomáš Mrkvička,Jan Radimský*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"配对交换置换检验"的新型非参数方法，用于比较两个相依预测变量对同一目标变量的相对解释力。该方法通过对称的组内交换机制和ECDF映射步骤，完美保留边缘分布与经验支撑，在重尾分布和类别稀疏等病态数据条件下仍能严格控制名义显著性水平并最大化统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典渐近方法（如Vuong贴近度检验、Hotelling-Williams检验）在重尾分布、极端类别稀疏等病态数据条件下经常失效。朴素置换检验破坏了相依预测变量的协方差结构，而配对自助法会引入人为重复值、导致度量空间压缩和类别遗漏。因此，亟需一种在有限样本下保持精确性且能处理相依预测变量的非参数检验方法。

Method: 提出"配对交换置换检验"（Paired Swap Permutation Test），基于零假设下的函数可交换性原理：对分类数据采用对称的组内交换机制，对连续数据引入经验累积分布函数（ECDF）映射步骤。这种基于copula的转置方法能够在避免引入重抽样重复值的前提下，完美保留边缘密度和经验支撑。

Result: 蒙特卡洛模拟表明，在标准方法变得灾难性宽松或病态保守的条件下，所提检验严格维持名义显著性水平并最大化统计功效。在高维意大利语名词-名词复合词语言学数据集上，该方法在传统分析方法固有失效的环境中成功提供了稳健、精确的统计推断。

Conclusion: 配对交换置换检验为比较相依预测变量的相对解释力提供了一种精确、稳健的非参数推断框架，无论在模拟实验还是真实高维语言学数据中，均展现出显著优于经典渐近方法和传统重抽样方法的性能。

Abstract: Comparing the relative explanatory power of two dependent predictors regarding a common target variable is a fundamental challenge across scientific disciplines. Classical asymptotic procedures, such as Vuong's closeness test or the Hotelling-Williams test, frequently collapse under pathological data conditions, including heavy-tailed distributions and extreme categorical sparsity. To bypass these limitations, practitioners often turn to non-parametric resampling. However, naive permutation tests destroy the natural covariance structure of dependent predictors, while the paired bootstrap evaluating variance around the alternative hypothesis and introducing artificial ties suffers from metric space compression and categorical omission, rendering it highly unreliable in finite samples.
  In this paper, we introduce the Paired Swap Permutation Test, a novel and exact non-parametric methodology. Grounded in the principle of functional exchangeability under the null hypothesis, our algorithm utilizes a symmetric within-subject swapping mechanism for categorical data, and introduces an Empirical Cumulative Distribution Function (ECDF) mapping step for continuous domains. This copula-based transposition perfectly preserves marginal densities and the empirical support without introducing resampling ties.
  Through extensive Monte Carlo simulations, we demonstrate that the proposed test strictly maintains the nominal significance level and maximizes statistical power under conditions where standard methods become catastrophically liberal or pathologically conservative. Finally, we apply the framework to a high-dimensional linguistic dataset of Italian noun-noun compounds, proving its capacity to deliver robust, exact inference in environments where conventional analytical methods inherently fail.

</details>


### [181] [Predicting disease severity and large-scale spread from coupled severity measurements and imperfect indicators: Application to beet yellows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26972)
*Baptiste Oger,César Martinez,François Joudelat,Samuel Soubeyrand,Lionel Benoit*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两步统计框架，利用间接指标（如遥感数据）预测局部疾病严重程度并重建大尺度疾病动态，可处理零膨胀和时空结构，并通过法国甜菜黄化病案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疾病管理中，直接测量往往稀疏且成本高昂，而间接指标（如遥感数据）虽有噪声但覆盖广。需要一种方法有效整合间接指标来预测局部疾病严重程度，并重建大尺度时空流行病动态。

Method: 采用两步法：(1) 基于多重随机森林的堆叠栅栏模型（stacked hurdle model），利用间接指标局部预测疾病严重程度，可处理零膨胀特性；(2) 半参数时空模型，基于第一步的预测结果重建大尺度流行病时空动态。方法设计通用且模块化。

Result: 以2019-2023年法国甜菜黄化病监测为案例，结合稀疏田间测量数据和卫星遥感数据，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的统计框架能够有效融合稀疏直接测量与广覆盖间接指标，实现从局部到区域尺度的疾病动态重建，对植物、人类及动物健康领域的疾病管理具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Whether in human, animal, or plant health, effective disease management requires the ability to characterize disease dynamics across space and time. In this context, integrating indirect indicators with broad spatio-temporal coverage, even when they are noisy, can provide valuable complementary information to direct measurements, which are often sparse because they are more costly or intrusive to collect. In this article, we propose a statistical framework to leverage such indirect indicators to predict disease severity at the individual or local-scale level and reconstruct large-scale disease dynamics. This two-step approach is able to account for the specific characteristics of disease severity observations, including zero inflation and spatio-temporal structure. The first step relies on a stacked hurdle model based on multiple random forests to locally predict disease severity from the available indirect indicators. In the second step a semi-parametric spatio-temporal model is used to reconstruct large-scale epidemiological dynamics over space and time from the indicators-based predictions. The proposed methodology is designed to be both generic and modular, and is illustrated by a case study in plant health. This case study focuses on the monitoring of sugar beet yellows disease in France between 2019 and 2023 by combining sparse field measurements and satellite-based remote sensing data.

</details>


### [182] [Conditional Leibniz Derivative Estimation with an Application to American Call Min-Options](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27046)
*Xingyu Ren,Michael C. Fu,Pierre L'Ecuyer*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 提出条件化莱布尼茨导数估计方法，通过递归条件化消除传统LR方法中似然比项，解决方差随输入维度线性增长问题，并在美式看涨最小期权中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莱布尼茨导数估计作为基于似然比(LR)的Monte Carlo方法，其方差通常高于扰动分析类方法，且会随随机输入维度线性增长。该问题限制了该方法在高维随机模型中的实用性，需要一种不依赖LR项的低方差导数估计技术。

Method: 提出递归条件化方法并与莱布尼茨导数估计框架结合，得到条件莱布尼茨估计量。该估计量不含似然比项，从而从根本上避免方差随输入维度增长的问题，同时形式简洁、易于实现。

Result: 将所提方法应用于美式看涨最小期权模型，仿真结果表明条件莱布尼茨估计量具有有效性和低方差性能。

Conclusion: 递归条件化方法能够有效消除莱布尼茨导数估计中因LR项导致的高方差问题，在高维随机模型中有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Leibniz derivative estimation is a Monte Carlo technique for estimating derivatives of a discontinuous sample performance in stochastic models with respect to parameters of interest. By combining the push-out likelihood ratio (LR) method with Leibniz integral rules, it generalizes a broad class of existing LR-based derivative estimators. However, as an LR-based method, its variance is often higher than that of perturbation analysis-based methods and may grow linearly with the dimension of the stochastic input whose distribution depends on the parameter. In this paper, we propose a recursive conditioning approach and combine it with the Leibniz derivative estimation framework. The resulting conditional Leibniz estimator does not involve LR terms and therefore is not subject to variance growth with the input dimension. It also has a simple form and is easy to implement. We apply the method to an American call min-option model, and simulation results show its effectiveness and low-variance performance.

</details>


### [183] [Estimation of High Dimensional Bounded Discrete Graphical Models via Regularized Generalized Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27142)
*Xuran Meng,Jingfei Zhang,Yi Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出有界离散图模型（BRIDGE），用于有限支撑的多元离散响应变量。通过正则化广义得分匹配估计器，无需计算归一化常数，并借助重新参数化解决损失几何的退化问题。理论层面在非凸目标下建立了群体分离性质，得到非渐近误差界和高维下的精确支撑恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多元计数数据的图模型在处理无界支撑的计数变量时，为保证归一化常数有限，通常需要对交互参数施加严格约束。本文旨在通过构造有界离散图模型，从根本上消除此类约束，同时保留在观测尺度上的可解释依赖结构。

Method: 1) 提出有界离散图模型，限定支撑为有限集，自然规避归一化常数问题。2) 开发正则化广义得分匹配估计器（BRIDGE），作为似然估计的无归一化替代方法，构建统一的估计方程系统，并用ℓ₁惩罚实现联合正则化。3) 针对损失几何在截距方向上的退化问题，引入重新参数化以恢复曲率并保证稳定计算。

Result: 理论结果：在非凸目标下建立了群体分离性质以替代全局凸性，得到非渐近估计误差界和高维设定下的精确支撑恢复保证。数值结果：模拟研究和真实数据分析表明，BRIDGE能准确恢复图结构，为高维离散图建模提供稳定且可解释的框架。

Conclusion: BRIDGE方法成功解决了有界离散图模型中归一化常数的约束问题，通过得分匹配避免了似然计算的困难，在理论和实践中均展现出良好的估计与结构恢复性能，为高维离散图建模提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Graphical models for multivariate count data are widely used to characterize conditional dependence structures. For count variables with unbounded support, however, ensuring a finite normalizing constant typically imposes restrictive constraints on interaction parameters. We propose bounded discrete graphical models for multivariate discrete responses with finite support, which remove such constraints by construction while retaining interpretable dependence on the observed scale. We develop a regularized generalized score matching estimator (BRIDGE), which provides a normalization-free surrogate for likelihood-based estimation. The approach yields a unified system of estimating equations for all parameters and enables joint regularization through an $\ell_1$ penalty. To address degeneracy in the loss geometry, we introduce a reparameterization that restores curvature along the intercept direction and facilitates stable computation. On the theoretical side, we analyze a nonconvex objective and establish a population separation property that replaces global convexity. This yields nonasymptotic estimation error bounds and exact support recovery in high-dimensional regimes. Simulation studies and real data analyses demonstrate that BRIDGE accurately recovers graph structure and provides a stable and interpretable framework for high-dimensional discrete graphical modeling.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [The Role of Input Dimensionality in the Emergence and Targeted Control of Adversarial Examples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26207)
*Nasrin Malekzadeh Goradel,Niccolo Pancino,Yaser Gholizade Atani,Benedetta Tondi,Giovanni Bellettini,Mauro Barni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了输入维度对对抗样本产生与定向控制的作用，发现高维输入是导致对抗样本易于构造的核心因素，且定向攻击与非定向攻击之间的扰动差距随维度升高而缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释深度神经网络对抗脆弱性的高维几何理论缺乏充分的实证检验，其假设条件在真实数据上的适用性尚不明确。作者旨在通过系统实证研究填补这一空白。

Method: 首先分析基于测度集中现象的理论框架的适用范围与局限，揭示真实图像类别存在超越理论假设的强经验局部化特性；随后在跨层次图像数据集上开展大规模实证评估，涵盖不同输入维度和多种网络架构。

Result: 实验一致表明，随着输入维度增加，对抗样本更易构造；高维几何特性导致定向攻击所需的额外扰动相对非定向攻击十分有限，且维度越高差距越小。

Conclusion: 高输入维度是决定对抗样本产生与定向控制的基础性因素，但这一现象究竟源于高维几何与数据分布的交互，还是深度网络架构本身的属性，仍是一个待解的开放问题。

Abstract: Several theoretical works have tried to explain the adversarial vulnerability of deep neural networks through properties of high-dimensional geometry. However, the assumptions underlying these works are rarely examined empirically, and systematic evidence remains limited. In this work, we present a systematic study of the role of input dimensionality in both the emergence and the targeted control of adversarial examples. We first analyse the scope and limitations of existing theoretical frameworks based on concentration of measure, showing that real image classes exhibit strong empirical localization, beyond what such theories typically assume. We then conduct an extensive empirical evaluation across hierarchical image datasets spanning a wide range of input dimensionalities and diverse neural architectures. Our results consistently show that adversarial examples become easier to construct as dimensionality increases. We also investigate how input dimensionality affects the additional difficulty of crafting targeted adversarial examples. In particular, we provide theoretical arguments showing that high-dimensional geometry implies that enforcing a specific target label entails only a limited additional distortion compared to untargeted attacks. We corroborate this insight through extensive experiments, demonstrating that the gap between targeted and untargeted perturbations remains small and further narrows as input dimensionality increases. While, taken together, our findings establish high input dimensionality as a fundamental factor underlying the emergence and targeted control of adversarial examples, whether this phenomenon primarily arises from the interplay between high-dimensional geometry and data distributions or from the architectural properties of deep neural networks remains an open question.

</details>


### [185] [A probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26457)
*Daniel Corrales,David Ríos Insua*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于状态空间模型的概率框架，用于在线测试时自适应问题。该框架统一刻画了参数学习、参数时间演化、先验调优和预测等关键环节，以应对训练与测试分布可能发生偏移的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在在线测试时自适应场景中，模型需要在测试阶段利用无标签数据持续适应可能存在的分布偏移（distributional shift）。现有方法缺乏一个统一的概率视角来系统性地处理参数更新、时序演化和不确定性量化，因此作者希望建立一个形式化的概率框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用状态空间建模（state-space modelling）架构，将测试时自适应问题纳入概率推断框架。在该框架下，模型参数的动态变化被建模为状态演化过程，结合观测数据（无标签测试样本）进行在线推断，从而形式化地表征参数学习、参数时间演化、先验调优和预测等组件。

Result: （摘要中未提供具体实验数据或量化结果，仅介绍了框架的理论构建。）

Conclusion: 该概率框架为在线测试时自适应提供了一种统一的建模视角，能够系统性地处理分布偏移下的参数更新与预测问题，有望为后续研究提供理论基础和算法设计指导。

Abstract: This paper presents a probabilistic framework for online test-time adaptation problems. In them, a model is trained on labeled data but must adapt to unlabeled data at test time under the assumption that training and test distributions potentially differ, that is, there might have been a distributional shift. The framework is based on a state-space modelling architecture from which parameter learning, parameter time evolution, prior tuning, and prediction can be characterized.

</details>


### [186] [XMSE-Aware Adaptive Empirical Bayes Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26975)
*Minghao Chen,Jiale Zheng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种XMSE感知的混合估计器，在最大似然(ML)和经验贝叶斯(EB)收缩之间进行插值，通过闭式oracle混合权重在二阶尺度上实现不劣于两者的性能，并提供了一致性插件实现及多种扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的核基EB估计在核与真实参数对齐不佳时，其二阶超额均方误差(XMSE)可能劣于ML估计。本文旨在将这一诊断转化为设计原则，构建一个能自动识别并规避核误指定问题的估计器。

Method: 提出XMSE感知的混合估计器，在ML和EB收缩之间进行插值。固定权重的XMSE为标量二次型，由此导出闭式oracle最优混合权重。基于有限样本XMSE近似构建插件实现，并证明其一致性及二阶oracle遗憾界。进一步将遗憾界迁移至固定权重风险曲线、阈值边界规则，并扩展至紧致核族及有限/增长核字典的高概率oracle界。

Result: 在有限脉冲响应仿真（含SURE调优、硬选择、迹校正基线）以及公开的Silverbox和Cascaded Tanks基准上，所提估计器在正则化有益时保留其大部分收益，在核误指定时向ML回退，并在基准上识别出有限样本的优势。

Conclusion: 通过XMSE感知的混合策略，成功实现了在核适当时利用EB收缩优势、核误指定时自动退回ML的自适应估计器，兼具理论保证和实证有效性。

Abstract: Empirical Bayes (EB) estimators can match the first-order asymptotic risk of maximum likelihood (ML) while behaving very differently at second order: recent excess mean squared error (XMSE) analysis shows that kernel-based EB estimation may be worse than ML when the kernel is poorly aligned with the true parameter. This paper turns that diagnostic into a design principle. We propose an XMSE-aware mixed estimator that interpolates between ML and EB shrinkage. Its fixed-weight XMSE is a scalar quadratic, yielding a closed-form oracle mixing weight that is no worse than both ML and the base EB estimator at the XMSE scale. A plug-in implementation based on finite-sample XMSE approximations is proved consistent, with a second-order oracle regret rate for an interior oracle weight. We further establish a transfer of the regret bound to the fixed-weight risk curve evaluated at the selected weight, a thresholded boundary rule, and extensions to compact kernel families and to finite and growing kernel dictionaries with high-probability oracle bounds. Finite impulse response simulations with SURE-tuned, hard-selection, and trace-corrected baselines, together with the public Silverbox and Cascaded Tanks benchmarks, show that the proposed estimator retains most of the benefit of regularization when it is helpful and retreats toward ML under kernel misspecification, with an identified finite-de analyzed on the benchmarks.

</details>


### [187] [Beyond Global Divergences: A Local-Mass Perspective on Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27090)
*Hanli Xu,Fengxiang He,Sarat Moka*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯推断中全局目标（如KL散度、ELBO）无法直接捕捉的局部质量行为，引入质量指数和正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL）两种数学工具，刻画贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量，并证明了比较两种KL方向下局部小球质量的绝对、相对和方向不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KL散度和ELBO等全局目标虽然广泛用于贝叶斯推断中度量分布差异，但它们无法直接捕捉分布的局部质量（local-mass）行为——即概率质量在小球邻域内的衰减尺度。理解这一局部行为对于深入认识贝叶斯更新的局部几何性质、奇异组件存在时的分布逼近等具有重要意义。

Method: 作者引入两个核心数学工具：(1) 质量指数（Mass Index），用于记录局部质量的多项式和对数衰减尺度；(2) 正则化扩展KL散度（RE-KL），一种集合局部化的散度，可在存在奇异组件时被公式化。利用质量指数刻画贝叶斯更新如何改变局部质量（幂-对数似然因子显式平移；参数依赖支撑集或其光滑软化通过邻近参数值的剩余质量改变局部尺度）。利用局部RE-KL证明绝对、相对和方向不等式，用于比较两种KL方向下的局部小球质量。

Result: 通过质量指数揭示了：(1) 幂-对数似然因子会显式平移质量指数；(2) 参数依赖支撑集或其光滑软化会改变局部衰减尺度。利用局部RE-KL证明了绝对、相对和方向三类不等式，系统比较了两种KL方向（KL(q||p)与KL(p||q)）下的局部小球质量。实验提供了受控的局部行为图示说明。

Conclusion: 该工作为贝叶斯推断中的局部质量行为提供了系统的局部理论解释，质量指数和RE-KL两种工具能有效刻画全局目标所忽略的局部衰减特性，对理解贝叶斯更新的局部几何结构具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: Global objectives, such as KL divergence and ELBO, are widely used in Bayesian inference for measuring distributional discrepancy. This paper studies their local-mass behaviour that is not directly captured by such objectives. We introduce and use two mathematical tools: (1) Mass Index for recording the polynomial and logarithmic decay scales of local mass, and (2) regularised extended KL (RE-KL), a set-localised divergence that can be formulated in the presence of singular components. Mass Indices help characterise how Bayesian updating changes local mass: (1) power-log likelihood factors shift it explicitly, and (2) parameter-dependent supports, or their smooth softenings, may change the local scale through the amount of mass that remains near the parameter value. Using local RE-KL, we prove absolute, relative, and directional inequalities for comparing local small-ball masses under the two KL directions. Together, these results provide a local theoretical account of local mass behaviour. Experiments provide controlled illustrations of the local behaviour. Code is available at https://github.com/Forsythia0604/Local-Mass-Framework.

</details>


### [188] [Ribbon: Scalable Approximation and Robust Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27269)
*Graham Gibson,John Tipton,Kellin Rumsey,Natalie Klein*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Ribbon 是一种可扩展的近似方法，通过影响函数线性化替代重复模型重拟合，近似 Dirichlet 重加权自助法的不确定性估计，仅需事后线性代数运算，在保持校准性的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂高维或误设模型的预测不确定性量化面临计算困境：全贝叶斯方法和自助法虽能提供有原则的不确定性估计，但需要后验采样或重复模型重拟合，对现代机器学习模型而言计算成本过高。因此需要一种既能保持统计优良性又可扩展的近似方案。

Method: 提出 Ribbon 方法：围绕单一拟合模型进行影响函数线性化，保留贝叶斯自助法的一阶数据重加权结构；引入浓度参数控制不确定性尺度，可通过验证数据校准；仅需事后线性代数运算，避免重复训练。

Result: 理论证明 Ribbon 在正确似然设定下渐近等价于平坦先验的 Laplace 近似，在误设下恢复稳健的 sandwich 协方差；在合成回归、MNIST 分类和 California Housing 基准实验中，Ribbon 提供了有竞争力的预测性能，并在多种场景下改善了校准性。

Conclusion: Ribbon 成功在统计严谨性与计算可行性之间取得平衡，为大规模机器学习模型的不确定性量化提供了一种实用且理论有保障的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliably quantifying predictive uncertainty is difficult for complex, high-dimensional, or misspecified models. Both fully Bayesian and bootstrap resampling methods provide principled uncertainty estimates but are often too expensive for modern machine-learning models because they require posterior sampling or repeated model refitting. We introduce Ribbon, a scalable approximation to Dirichlet-reweighted bootstrap uncertainty. Ribbon replaces repeated refitting with an influence-function linearization around a single fitted model, preserving the first-order data-reweighting structure of the Bayesian bootstrap while requiring only post-hoc linear algebra. Ribbon approximates the Bayesian-bootstrap or weighted-likelihood-bootstrap refitting target. With a general concentration parameter, Ribbon gives a calibrated Dirichlet-reweighting family whose uncertainty scale can be tuned on validation data. We show that Ribbon is asymptotically equivalent to a flat-prior Laplace approximation under correct likelihood specification and recovers the robust sandwich covariance under misspecification. Across synthetic regression, MNIST classification, and California Housing benchmarks, Ribbon provides competitive predictive performance and improved calibration in several settings while avoiding repeated model retraining.

</details>


### [189] [When are likely answers right? On Sequence Probability and Correctness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27359)
*Johannes Zenn,Jonas Geiping*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统量化了语言模型解码中序列概率与正确性之间的关系，发现序列概率在同一数据集内能预测正确性，但通过调整解码方法或超参数来提升序列概率并不能可靠地提高准确率，且对同一提示的多次响应，序列概率也不是正确性的良好指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多解码方法本质上是在局部或全局层面将概率质量移向模型认为更可能的输出。然而，一个根本性问题尚未被充分回答：序列概率（即给定提示下续写的条件概率）究竟在何时与正确性对齐？作者旨在系统量化这一关系，为解码方法的选择和设计提供理论依据。

Method: 作者在四个层面上系统量化序列概率与正确性的关系：(1) 跨不同解码方法（如贪心解码、束搜索、温度采样等）；(2) 同一方法内跨不同超参数设置；(3) 同一数据集内跨不同提示-答案对；(4) 同一提示的多次重复响应。实验覆盖多种模型和基准测试。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 在固定数据集内，序列概率较高通常能预测正确性；(2) 但这种关系不能泛化到解码决策层面——通过改变超参数或解码方法来提升序列概率并不能可靠地提高准确率；(3) 对于同一提示的多次响应，序列概率不是正确性的良好指标。

Conclusion: 这些发现阐明了何时可以通过解码来改善正确性、何时不能，并为解码策略、自一致性（self-consistency）以及无验证器的自我改进提供了实用的指导。

Abstract: Many decoding methods for large language models can be understood as shifting probability mass toward outputs that are more likely under the model, either locally at the token level or globally at the sequence level. Therefore, their success depends on a fundamental question: when does sequence probability, that is, the conditional probability of a continuation given a prompt, actually align with correctness? In this paper, we set out to quantify this relationship across decoding methods, models, and benchmarks at four levels: across decoding methods, across hyperparameters within a method, across prompt-answer pairs within a dataset, and across repeated responses to the same prompt. We find that higher sequence probability is often predictive of correctness across prompt-answer pairs within a fixed dataset. However, this relationship does not generally transfer to decoding decisions: increasing sequence probability by changing hyperparameters or methods does not reliably improve accuracy. Further, sequence probability is not a good indicator of correctness for responses to the same prompt. These findings clarify when decoding can and cannot be expected to improve correctness, and provide practical guidance for decoding, self-consistency, and verifier-free self-improvement.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Detecting and Controlling Sycophancy with Cascading Linear Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26155)
*Maty Bohacek,Rishub Jain,Nicholas Dufour,Thomas Leung,Chris Bregler,Roma Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代式数据生成流程，通过构建特征程度与行为呈线性关系的级联样本（而非简单二分类对比对），实现激活转向中特征与行为的更好解耦。以语言模型的谄媚（sycophancy）行为为例，证明了该方法发现的线性特征子空间在检测、评分和鲁棒转向方面，可匹配或超越LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，且计算成本更低、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活转向方法依赖对比样本对来定位行为相关特征，但简单二分类样本对难以有效解耦级联的线性特征，导致特征检测和模型行为控制的可靠性受限。本文希望超越二元对比，通过构建特征程度线性变化的样本序列，实现更精细的特征解耦与行为操控。

Method: 提出一种迭代数据生成流程（cascading data generation pipeline），生成特征程度与目标行为呈线性缩放关系的级联样本。利用这些样本定位谄媚行为的线性特征子空间，基于该子空间选择模型激活进行行为检测、确定性评分和鲁棒激活转向，并与LLM-as-a-judge及系统提示基线进行比较。

Result: 级联样本发现的谄媚特征形成线性可分子空间，所选模型激活比基线方法更清晰地对应目标行为；在检测、确定性评分和鲁棒转向任务上，匹配或超越LLM-as-a-judge和系统提示基线，同时计算需求更低、可解释性保证更强。

Conclusion: 通过级联线性特征样本替代简单二分类对比对，可以更有效地解耦行为相关特征，实现对模型行为（如谄媚）的可靠检测与鲁棒控制，为激活转向方法提供了更高效、更可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Interpreting and controlling model behaviors through activation steering methods requires many pairs of contrastive samples that clearly exhibit desired or undesired behavior. These data pairs determine the degree to which interpretability frameworks can reliably detect model features responsible for a behavior, and therefore the ability to steer models toward or away from such behavior. In this work, we present an iterative data generation pipeline that isolates cascading linear features responsible for a behavior. Specifically, we show how moving beyond simple binary pairs of samples, and instead isolating samples that show degrees of features that scale linearly with behavior, allows for better disentanglement of features. We focus on detecting and steering away from sycophancy -- the tendency of language models to prioritize user validation. We demonstrate that sycophancy features discovered through cascading samples form linearly separable subspaces, and allow for selection of model activations that more clearly correspond to the desired behavior than baseline approaches. We also evaluate their ability to enable detection, deterministic scoring, and robust steering, and see that they either match or outperform LLM-as-a-judge and system prompting baselines while providing lower computational demand and more interpretability guarantees. Code & Data: https://cascading-feats.github.io/

</details>


### [191] [Life After Benchmark Saturation: A Case Study of CORE-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26158)
*Nitya Nadgir,Sayash Kapoor,Kangheng Liu,Peter Kirgis,Matilda Orona,Stephan Rabanser,Tilman Bayer,Abhishek Shetty,Yue Ling,Derrick Chan-Sew,Rumi Nakagawa,Saiteja Utpala,Zachary S. Siegel,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了以准确率饱和即退役基准测试的做法，提出应从六个额外维度评估智能体性能，并以CORE-Bench Hard为案例，展示了即使准确率饱和后，基准测试在衡量效率、可靠性、模型/脚手架性能及人机协作提升方面仍具价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基准测试评估范式过度依赖准确率指标，一旦准确率饱和便退役并替换为更难版本。这种做法忽视了构念效度（如捷径）、分布外泛化、效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架相对贡献、人机协作提升等六个关键维度的评估机会。

Method: 以CORE-Bench Hard（科学代码计算可复现性基准）为案例研究：1）揭示构念效度威胁，推出改进版CORE-Bench v1.1及分布外任务套件CORE-Bench OOD；2）在准确率饱和后继续测量效率、可靠性、模型和脚手架性能；3）通过小规模随机实验量化人机协作在真实计算可复现任务上的提升效果。

Result: 1）在CORE-Bench Hard中发现难以预见的构念效度威胁；2）CORE-Bench v1.1在准确率饱和后仍能有效区分效率、可靠性、模型与脚手架性能；3）人机协作实验显示约两倍的统计显著加速效果，且该效果可能因纯人工组五分之一任务超时未完成而被低估。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一套比以准确率为中心的评估范式更严谨的替代方案，主张基准测试应在多维度上持续提供有意义的洞察，而非仅因准确率饱和就匆忙退役。

Abstract: When a benchmark's accuracy saturates, it is often retired and replaced with a more challenging version. We show that this approach privileges accuracy and misses the opportunity to study six other key dimensions of agent performance: construct validity issues such as shortcuts, out-of-distribution generalizability, efficiency, reliability, the relative importance of the model versus the scaffold, and uplift from human-agent collaboration. We use CORE-Bench Hard, a benchmark for computational reproducibility of scientific code, as a case study to demonstrate that measuring agents along these dimensions yields meaningful insights into agent performance even after accuracy saturates. First, we surface threats to construct validity in CORE-Bench Hard that are difficult to anticipate with less capable agents. We introduce an improved benchmark, CORE-Bench v1.1, and an out-of-distribution task suite, CORE-Bench OOD. Second, we find that despite accuracy saturation, CORE-Bench v1.1 remains useful for measuring efficiency, reliability, model performance, and scaffold performance. Finally, we conduct a small-scale randomized experiment to measure uplift from human-agent collaboration on real-world computational reproducibility tasks. We find a statistically significant speedup by about a factor of two -- likely underestimated due to one-fifth of human-only reproductions reaching the time limit before completing -- and describe various other findings. Together, our contributions present a more rigorous alternative to the dominant accuracy-centric evaluation paradigm.

</details>


### [192] [Refusal Lives Downstream of Persona in Chat Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26161)
*Viola Zhong,Qirui Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了指令微调聊天模型中"拒绝"与"人格"两条激活方向并非独立，而是存在交互：服从型人格方向在后期层对拒绝方向起门控作用，干预人格方向可大幅抑制或恢复拒绝行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究将激活空间中的拒绝方向和人格方向视为独立机制分别研究，作者旨在揭示两者之间的交互关系——人格特质如何调控模型是否表达拒绝行为。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上提取服从型人格方向与拒绝方向，通过激活引导（steering）和投影消除（projection）进行因果干预，分别在早期层和后期层操作，观察拒绝率变化。

Result: 服从型人格引导使Llama的拒绝率从97%骤降至2%；在后期层重新引入拒绝方向可部分恢复拒绝，但在早期层则不能；在后期层窗口投影消除人格方向可恢复基线水平的拒绝，而投影随机方向无此效果。

Conclusion: 拒绝行为在后期层表达阶段被人格方向门控，位于拒绝方向被计算完成的下游。将拒绝视为孤立方向会忽略其对人格的依赖性。

Abstract: Linear directions in activation space have been identified for both refusal and persona traits in instruction-tuned chat models, but the two have been studied as separate mechanisms. We show they interact: a compliant persona gates refusal. In Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, we extract a compliant model-persona direction and a refusal direction and intervene on both. Compliant persona steering suppresses refusal -- in Llama, the refusal rate falls from 97% to 2%. Reintroducing the refusal direction partially restores refusal at late layers but not at early ones. Projecting out the persona direction in a late-layer window restores it to baseline; projecting out a random direction does not. Refusal is therefore gated at the late-layer expression stage, downstream of where it is computed. Treating refusal as a single isolated direction misses its dependence on persona.

</details>


### [193] [AlgoEvolve: LLM-driven Meta-evolution of Algorithmic Trading Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26173)
*Dhruv Sharma,Gautam Shroff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AlgoEvolve，一个基于大语言模型的进化框架，用于在算法交易领域自动生成、评估和迭代改进可执行交易策略，并引入元进化外循环来优化指导程序合成的提示词。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的语义进化研究多集中于静态编程基准测试，尚未拓展到算法交易这类噪声大、非平稳、高度不连续的复杂动态环境。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索LLM在持续变化的真实世界领域中进行程序合成与自适应优化的潜力。

Method: 提出 AlgoEvolve 框架：(1) 内循环——LLM作为语义变异算子，以Python代码形式生成交易策略，通过严格回测协议评估，迭代进化出具有自适应能力的策略；(2) 外循环——元进化机制，进化内循环中用于引导LLM程序合成的提示词，自动发现更优的搜索启发式，以平衡探索与利用、减少零交易失败。

Result: 系统涌现出 regime-adaptive 策略逻辑（如自主切换交易规则）；元进化外循环发现的提示词在探索-利用平衡和减少零交易失败方面持续优于人工设计的初始指令。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义进化是一种在复杂环境中进行持续程序合成的可行方法，在算法交易等非平稳领域展现出良好的自适应能力与应用前景。

Abstract: Recent work shows that Large Language Models (LLMs) can act as semantic mutation operators for the evolutionary discovery of programs and proofs. Most current applications focus on static coding benchmarks. We extend this paradigm to algorithmic trading. This domain is uniquely challenging because it is noisy, non-stationary, and highly discontinuous. We present AlgoEvolve, an LLM-driven evolutionary framework that generates, evaluates, and iteratively improves executable trading strategies. These strategies are expressed as Python code and evaluated through a rigorous testing protocol. Across multiple experiments, the system exhibits emergent regime-adaptive strategy logic, including autonomous shifts in trading rules. We further introduce a meta-evolutionary outer loop that evolves the prompts guiding program synthesis in the inner loop. This outer loop discovers improved search heuristics. These heuristics balance exploration and exploitation while reducing zero-trade failures. They consistently outperform initial human-designed instructions. The results demonstrate that LLM-based semantic evolution provides a viable approach for continual program synthesis in complex environments.

</details>


### [194] [Agentic Analysis for Agentic Infrastructure: An LLM-Powered Pipeline for Comparative Governance of DAO and Corporate AI Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26203)
*Yutian Wang,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于LLM的大规模治理话语比较分析流水线，通过对ERC-8004（无许可链上）与Google A2A（企业主导）两种Agent互操作性标准的4,323条治理参与记录进行分析，发现治理形式影响议题焦点，但两种模式在参与不平等和社区碎片化程度上相似；无许可环境中的话语对齐更密集，表明开放治理在去中心化参与下反而促进议题收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI Agent协议激增，塑造其互操作性标准的治理结构在实证层面仍缺乏充分研究。现有方法难以对大规模治理话语中的社会技术权力结构进行系统化比较分析，亟需可扩展的分析工具来填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了一条LLM驱动的比较分析流水线，整合了三项核心技术：①自动化标注（LLM辅助编码）；②神经主题建模（neural topic modeling）；③多层网络分析（multi-layer network analysis）。以ERC-8004和Google A2A为对比案例，对4,323条治理参与记录进行话语分析，考察制度设计如何塑造议题优先级和社区结构。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 治理形式确实影响实质性议题焦点；(2) 两种治理模式在参与不平等程度和社区碎片化方面表现相当；(3) 无许可环境（ERC-8004）中的话语对齐密度更高，表明开放治理虽具去中心化参与特征，却可能促进更强的议题收敛性。

Conclusion: LLM辅助方法能有效推进技术治理的实证研究，为设计更公平的Agent AI标准提供启示。研究数据与代码已完全开源。

Abstract: As AI agent protocols proliferate, the governance structures shaping their interoperability standards remain empirically underexamined. We introduce an LLM-powered comparative pipeline for large-scale governance discourse analysis, integrating automated annotation, neural topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to study socio-technical power structures at scale. We validate it on two contrasting standards for agent interoperability: ERC-8004 (permissionless, on-chain) and Google A2A (corporate-led). Analyzing 4,323 governance participation records, we combine LLM-assisted coding, topic modeling, and multi-layer network analysis to examine how institutional design shapes thematic priorities and community structure. We find that while governance form influences substantive focus, both regimes exhibit comparable levels of participation inequality and community fragmentation. Discourse alignment is denser in the permissionless setting, suggesting that open governance may foster greater thematic convergence despite decentralized participation. These findings illustrate how LLM-assisted methods can advance the empirical study of technology governance, with implications for designing more equitable agentic AI standards. All data and code are openly available.

</details>


### [195] [Knowledge-augmented Agentic AI for Mental Health Medication Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26205)
*Huizi Yu,Jian Liu,Wenkong Wang,Lingyao Li,Jiayan Zhou,Zhaoqian Xue,Xiang Li,Xinxin Lin,Zhiying Liang,Zhuoru Wu,Siyuan Ma,Xin Ma,Lizhou Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种溯源感知、基于知识图谱的多智能体框架，整合了Reddit帖子、WebMD评论和FDA不良事件报告系统（FAERS）的数据，用于九种抗抑郁药的安全性分析。研究发现患者生成数据与监管报告形成部分独立的安全信号，社区来源数据中部分不良事件的报告时间比FDA记录早数百天。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神科药物的安全性信息分散在权威但抽象的监管不良事件记录和具体但未经验证的患者叙述之间。简单合并这两类来源可能混淆证据与轶闻，尤其在精神科领域，缺乏上下文的药物信息可能放大恐惧、安慰剂效应和药物不依从。因此需要一种既能整合多源数据、又能保持来源可追溯性的方法。

Method: 构建了一个基于知识图谱的多智能体框架：（1）收集466,525条Reddit帖子、60,782条WebMD评论及20年FAERS记录，覆盖九种抗抑郁药；（2）使用大型语言模型进行实体识别，经医生标注验证，药物实体F1达0.969，病症实体F1达0.973；（3）对社区平台数据与监管数据进行一致性比较（Jaccard相似度）；（4）基于Neo4j构建知识图谱，使用ATC-N、ICD-10和MedDRA标准词汇表进行术语规范化，保证每条声明可追溯，区分监管事实与患者经验。

Result: 两个社区平台（Reddit与WebMD）之间的一致性远高于它们各自与监管报告的一致性，Jaccard相似度最高达0.905，表明患者生成数据构成部分独立的安全信号。以舍曲林为例，多项不良事件在社区来源中出现的时间比FDA记录日期早数百天。

Conclusion: 溯源感知的多源整合为精神科药物信息提供了更可审计的途径，其有用性和患者获益有待前瞻性验证。

Abstract: Patients increasingly seek medication information online, yet safety knowledge for psychiatric drugs is split between regulatory adverse-event records, which are authoritative but abstract, and patient narratives, which are experience-near but unvalidated. Integrating them without conflating evidence and anecdote is especially consequential in psychiatry, where poorly contextualised information can amplify fear, nocebo responses, and non-adherence. Here we develop a provenance-aware, knowledge-graph-based multi-agent framework unifying 466,525 Reddit posts, 60,782 WebMD reviews, and twenty years of U.S. FDA Adverse Event Reporting System records for nine antidepressants. A large-language-model entity-recognition pipeline benchmarked against physician annotations reached highest F1 scores of 0.969 for medications and 0.973 for conditions. The two community platforms were far more concordant with each other (overlap up to a Jaccard similarity of 0.905) than with regulatory reports, indicating that patient-generated data form a partly independent safety signal. For sertraline, many adverse events appeared in community sources hundreds of days before the corresponding FDA date. A Neo4j knowledge graph grounded in ATC-N, ICD-10, and MedDRA vocabularies preserves provenance, keeping every claim traceable and regulatory facts distinct from patient experience. These results establish source-aware integration as a route to more auditable psychiatric medication information, with usefulness and patient benefit to be tested prospectively.

</details>


### [196] [Accelerating Skill Assessment in Chess: A Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26267)
*Tianyuan Zhou,Zhizheng Fu,Tianming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DD-Elo（漂移-扩散增强Elo评分系统）的新型棋手技能评估框架，其灵感来源于认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM）。该方法通过将技能表现建模为决策过程，融合了每一步走棋的细粒度数据，从而能够捕捉快速的技能波动。数学推导证明DD-Elo与传统Elo系统之间的偏差是有界的，实验表明其在技能变化响应速度上显著优于传统Elo。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Elo评分系统虽然是竞技象棋配对的金标准，但存在响应滞后的固有缺陷——它仅依赖比赛结果，忽略了走棋过程中的细粒度质量信息。然而，将逐步走棋信息纳入评分调整面临两大挑战：走棋数据噪声大、棋局状态空间极其庞大。

Method: 作者借鉴认知神经科学中的漂移扩散模型（DDM），提出DD-Elo框架。其核心思路是将棋手的技能表达建模为一个决策过程，利用走棋层面的数据（move-level data）来捕捉快速的技能波动。论文还提供了严格的数学推导，证明了DD-Elo与传统Elo系统之间的偏差是有界的，从而保证了理论一致性。

Result: 大量实验表明，DD-Elo在适应技能变化方面比传统Elo系统更快。同时该系统具有可解释性强、响应迅速、向后兼容等优势。

Conclusion: DD-Elo为国际象棋评分生态系统提供了一种可解释、高响应性且向后兼容的解决方案，有望弥补传统Elo系统响应滞后的不足。代码已在GitHub公开。

Abstract: Rating systems such as Elo serve as the gold standard for matchmaking in competitive chess. However, they inherently suffer from response lag due to their exclusive reliance on match outcomes, neglecting the granular quality of gameplay. Nevertheless, incorporating move-by-move information into rating adjustments presents a significant challenge given the substantial noise and the vastness of the game-state space. To address this, we propose the Drift-Diffusion-Enhanced Elo Rating System (DD-Elo), a novel skill assessment framework inspired by the drift diffusion model (DDM) from cognitive neuroscience. By modeling skill expression as a decision-making process, our model integrates move-level data to capture rapid skill fluctuations. We provide a rigorous mathematical derivation proving that DD-Elo maintains a bounded deviation from the traditional Elo system, ensuring theoretical alignment. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DD-Elo adapts to skill changes faster than Elo. Our findings suggest that DD-Elo offers an explainable, highly responsive, and backward-compatible solution for chess rating ecosystems. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aquila-zhou1/DD-Elo .

</details>


### [197] [Governing Actions, Not Agents: Institutional Attestation as a Governance Model for Autonomous AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26298)
*Jakob Salfeld-Nebgen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对自主AI代理的计算治理模型：AI代理保留完全的规划和推理自主权，但对高风险执行动作没有直接执行权限；执行需依赖由独立权威来源逐一证实的先决条件，并通过确定性策略进行评估，所有决策记录在防篡改日志中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理可能开始执行不可逆的高风险动作（如临床处方、生产软件部署），而人类制度治理强大自主行为者的方式并非监控其推理过程，而是要求在关键行动节点提供独立验证的证据。这一治理模式尚未在AI代理系统中得到形式化的计算建模。

Method: 将人类制度的治理模式形式化为AI代理的计算治理模型，核心机制包括：(1) 代理保留推理自主权但无高风险动作执行权；(2) 执行以多个独立权威来源分别证实的先决条件为前提；(3) 先决条件通过密码学方式绑定到声明的意图；(4) 由确定性策略评估；(5) 所有决策记录在防篡改日志中，支持独立复验。提供概念验证实现，并以软件部署和临床处方为例进行说明。

Result: 完成了概念验证实现，并通过软件部署和临床处方两个应用场景展示了该治理模型的可行性和适用性，证明该模型能够在保留AI代理推理自主性的同时，对高风险行动施加有效的制度性约束。

Conclusion: 借鉴人类制度治理模式、以独立验证证据为核心的计算治理模型，能够在不过度限制AI代理自主性的前提下，为高风险、不可逆行动提供有效的安全保障和可审计性。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents may begin to perform consequential, irreversible actions such as clinical prescribing and production software deployment. This paper observes that human institutions have governed powerful autonomous actors not by monitoring their reasoning but by requiring independently attested evidence at the point of consequential action. We formalise this institutional pattern as a computational governance model for AI agent systems. Under the proposed model, an agent retains full autonomy over planning and reasoning but holds no execution authority over designated high-risk actions. Execution is conditional on preconditions that are each independently attested by a separate authoritative source, cryptographically bound to a declared intent, and evaluated by a deterministic policy. Decisions are recorded in a tamper-evident log amenable to independent re-verification. We present a proof-of-concept implementation and illustrate the model with examples from software deployment and clinical prescribing.

</details>


### [198] [COrigami: An AI Pipeline for Co-Designing Flat-Foldable Visually Recognisable Origami](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26299)
*Tom Zahavy,Shaobo Hou,Thomas Tumiel,James Doran,Francesco Faccio,Xidong Feng,Alex Havrilla,Igor Khytryi,Chenglei Li,Lisa Schut,Vivek Veeriah,Arijan Abrashi,Michał Kosmulski,Robert J. Lang,Nick Robinson,Brandon Wong,Marcus Chiam,Gloria Fang,Satinder Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出COrigami，一个端到端的AI驱动流水线，能够根据自然语言描述生成满足平折几何约束的折纸折痕图案，并通过强化学习和自主美学评估回路优化生成结果，实现数学严谨的人机协同创作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在可验证解的问题上取得了显著成功，但在同时满足严格几何约束和主观视觉美学的物理艺术生成方面仍面临挑战。折纸领域具有严格的数学框架（平折条件），是将算法优化与艺术设计结合的理想试验场。

Method: COrigami采用五阶段流水线：(1) 从自然语言生成语义骨架图；(2) 计算基础堆积（base packing）；(3) 求解平折折痕图案；(4) 对平折折痕图案进行塑形；(5) 利用基于自主美学评估回路的强化学习对生成模型进行精炼优化。

Result: 该系统能够充当高效的协作助手，生成结构化的起始设计，供人类艺术家进一步扩展和塑形，实现了多目标物理约束下的可靠数学共创。

Conclusion: 通过将算法优化与自主美学评判相结合，COrigami展示了AI系统如何在满足多目标物理约束的前提下实现可靠、数学上严格的人机共创，为计算折纸领域的AI辅助设计开辟了新路径。

Abstract: While generative AI has achieved remarkable success in solving problems with verifiable solutions, generating physical art that satisfies both strict geometric constraints and subjective visual aesthetics remains a challenge. This paper presents an approach to tackle these difficulties in the domain of computational origami, a mathematically rigid environment that grounds artistic design within the equations of flat foldability. We present COrigami, an end-to-end AI-driven pipeline that assists the design cycle by generating crease patterns from natural language. Our pipeline involves generating a semantic stick figure, computing a base packing, solving for a flat-foldable crease pattern, shaping the flat-folded crease pattern, and refining the generated model using reinforcement learning driven by an autonomous aesthetic evaluation loop. Our system acts as a highly effective collaborative assistant, generating structural starting points that human artists can further expand and shape. By integrating algorithmic optimisation with autonomous aesthetic critique, this work demonstrates how AI systems can satisfy multi-objective physical constraints to enable reliable, mathematically grounded co-creativity.

</details>


### [199] [The Verification Horizon: No Silver Bullet for Coding Agent Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26300)
*Binghai Wang,Chenlong Zhang,Dayiheng Liu,Jiajun Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Mouxiang Chen,Rongyao Fang,Siyuan Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuheng Jing,Zeyao Ma,Zeyu Cui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 随着基础模型推理能力增强，生成复杂代码解决方案不再困难，但可靠地验证这些方案反而成为更难的瓶颈。本文系统分析了验证信号的三个维度——可扩展性、忠实性和鲁棒性——并指出三者同时达成是核心挑战。通过四种奖励构造的实验，论文得出核心结论：不存在固定的奖励函数能在策略能力持续增长时保持有效，验证必须与生成器协同进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直觉认为验证比生成更容易，但在当前编码智能体时代，这一直觉被颠覆：生成复杂候选方案已非难事，但可靠验证却成为瓶颈。验证器永远只是人类意图的代理，非意图本身，这导致双重困难——意图本身的欠指定性和训练中代理-意图差距的扩大（表现为奖励黑客或信号饱和）。

Method: 论文提出从三个维度刻画验证信号质量：可扩展性（scalability）、忠实性（faithfulness）和鲁棒性（robustness），并研究了四种奖励构造方案：通用编码任务的测试验证器、前端任务的量规验证器、真实世界智能体任务中的用户作为验证器、以及长周期任务的自动化智能体验证器。论文在不同任务类型和策略能力水平上进行了深入分析和实验。

Result: 实验表明，有针对性的验证设计能有效抑制奖励黑客行为，提升任务完成质量，并在多个内部和公开基准上取得显著提升。

Conclusion: 核心观察是：没有固定的奖励函数能在策略能力持续增长时保持有效；验证必须与生成器协同进化（co-evolve）。

Abstract: A classical intuition holds that verifying a solution is easier than producing one. For today's coding agents, this intuition is being inverted: as foundation models develop stronger reasoning capabilities and engineering harnesses grow more sophisticated, generating complex candidate solutions is no longer difficult -- reliably verifying them has become the harder problem. Every verifier we can build is only a proxy for human intent, never the intent itself. This makes verification subject to a twofold difficulty: first, intent is underspecified by nature, making it inherently hard to faithfully check whether it has been fulfilled; second, during model training, optimization widens the gap between proxy and intent -- manifesting as reward hacking or signal saturation. To address this, we characterize the quality of verification signals along three dimensions -- scalability, faithfulness, and robustness -- and argue that achieving all three simultaneously is the central challenge. We further study four reward constructions: a test verifier for general coding tasks, a rubric verifier for frontend tasks, the user as verifier for real-world agent tasks, and an automated agent verifier for long-horizon tasks. Across different task types and policy capability levels, we conduct in-depth analysis and experiments on the core challenges of reward design and how to more effectively leverage reward signals. Experiments show that targeted verification design can effectively suppress reward hacking, improve task completion quality, and achieve significant gains across multiple internal and public benchmarks. These experiences collectively point to a core observation: no fixed reward function can remain effective as policy capability continues to grow; and verification must co-evolve with the generator.

</details>


### [200] [How Do Tool-Augmented LLM Agents Perform on Real-World Energy Analytics Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26346)
*David Akinpelu,Akintonde Abbas,Rereloluwa Alimi,Ayodeji Lana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对工具增强型LLM智能体在真实能源市场分析任务中的表现进行了实证研究，构建了包含243个专家策划问题的评估基准，涵盖市场数据检索、知识检索与解释、高级定量建模三大类，并采用多维度评估协议，比较了闭源与开源模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体基准测试在金融、编程、法律、药物发现等领域已广泛应用，但能源领域的评估仍局限于静态知识回忆，缺乏对实时数据检索、专业监管与市场知识以及多步定量推理能力的评估。这一空白对于需要高度专业化能力的能源行业十分关键。

Method: 构建了包含243个专家策划问题的评估环境，分为三类：(1) 市场数据检索与分析；(2) 知识检索与解释；(3) 高级定量建模与决策分析。智能体配备可配置领域工具套件，包括美国主要ISO实时电力市场API、监管案卷搜索、公用事业费率数据库、资产优化模型以及基于RAG的能源市场文档检索。评估采用多维度评分协议，从方法正确性、答案准确性、属性对齐和来源有效性四个维度进行评判，并通过类别感知路由匹配评分标准与问题类型。

Result: 对闭源和开源LLM均进行了评估，提供了在高风险专业领域中模型能力与领域工具如何交互的比较分析。关键实验产物已公开发布以支持可复现性和未来研究。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了能源领域智能体评估的空白，为工具增强型LLM在真实能源市场分析中的表现建立了首个系统性基准，展示了领域专用工具与模型能力之间的交互效应，为未来能源领域AI应用研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks have emerged across general-purpose and domain-specific settings, including finance, coding, law, and drug discovery, yet energy-domain evaluations remain largely limited to static knowledge recall. This is a critical gap for a sector that requires live data retrieval, specialized regulatory and market knowledge, and multi-step quantitative reasoning under real-world constraints.
  We present an empirical study of tool-augmented LLM agents on real-world energy market analytics tasks. Our evaluation environment includes 243 expert-curated problems across three categories: (1) Market Data Retrieval and Analysis, (2) Knowledge Retrieval and Interpretation, and (3) Advanced Quantitative Modeling and Decision Analytics. Tasks include price and demand analysis, tariff impact modeling, asset revenue and returns estimation, hedging strategy analysis, and optimization modeling, with problems spanning multiple difficulty levels.
  Agents are equipped with a configurable suite of domain tools, including live electricity market APIs for major U.S. ISOs, regulatory docket search, utility tariff databases, asset optimization models, and retrieval-augmented generation over energy market documents. We assess agent responses using a multi-dimensional evaluation protocol that scores approach correctness, answer accuracy, attribute alignment, and source validity, with category-aware routing to match scoring criteria to question type. We evaluate both closed-source and open-source LLMs, providing a comparative analysis of how model capability and domain tooling interact in a high-stakes professional domain. Key artifacts are publicly released to support reproducibility and future research.

</details>


### [201] [What We are Missing in Multimodal LLM Evaluation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26348)
*Po-han Li,Shenghui Chen,Sandeep Chinchali,Ufuk Topcu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有MLLM评估基准多局限于孤立任务，缺乏对跨模态信息整合能力的考察。本文审视当前评估手段，识别出时空一致性、物理世界理解、多模态一致性和选择性注意力等关键评估缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型能力快速发展，但评估方法严重滞后——现有基准只能测量孤立任务表现，无法揭示模型是否真正实现了跨模态信息整合，这阻碍了对多模态智能真实进展的衡量和能力边界的界定。

Method: 通过对现有MLLM评估基准的分类体系（taxonomy）进行系统回顾与审视，识别当前评估范式中的结构性缺失，提出需要填补的关键评估维度。

Result: 识别出四个核心评估缺口：时间-空间一致性（temporal-spatial coherence）、物理世界理解（physical world understanding）、多模态一致性（multimodal consistency）和选择性注意力（selective attention）。

Conclusion: 填补上述四个评估缺口对于衡量多模态智能的真实进展、揭示模型能力边界至关重要，当前的评估基准在这些维度上存在明显不足。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) can process diverse inputs, e.g., text, images, audio, and video, and generate textual responses. While their capabilities have advanced rapidly, evaluation of such models has not kept pace. Most existing evaluation benchmarks are limited to isolated tasks and reveal little about whether a model integrates information across modalities. We examine current means for evaluating MLLMs and review the existing benchmark taxonomy to identify gaps, including temporal-spatial coherence, physical world understanding, multimodal consistency, and selective attention. Addressing these gaps is essential for measuring real progress in multimodal intelligence and exposing capability boundaries.

</details>


### [202] [OpenFinGym: A Verifiable Multi-Task Gym Environment for Evaluating Quant Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26350)
*Kaicheng Zhang,Wen Ge,Lei Jiang,Weixin Yang,Jordan Langham-Lopez,Jialin Yu,Lukasz Szpruch,Hao Ni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenFinGym 是一个统一的量化金融智能体开发竞技场，将预测、市场生成、实时交易和欺诈检测等任务整合到单一接口下，并提供自动化任务构建管线、容器化运行时、纸面交易引擎等基础设施，解决现有基准测试碎片化、缺乏金融真实性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体在量化金融中的应用评估高度碎片化：各基准仅覆盖单一孤立任务，忽视了金融工作流固有的多阶段、相互依赖特性（预测→策略构建→风险管理→交易）。这种碎片化导致对智能体能力的评估过于乐观，无法暴露其在泛化能力、真实市场交互和金融意义上决策的弱点。

Method: 提出了OpenFinGym统一竞技场，核心设计包括：(1) 覆盖预测、市场生成、实时交易、欺诈检测四类任务的统一执行与验证接口；(2) 自动化任务构建管线，将量化金融论文转化为可执行任务包；(3) 容器化运行时配合宿主机验证器服务，支持可扩展的智能体rollout并防止训练-测试数据泄露；(4) 低延迟数据流设计的纸面交易引擎；(5) 针对长周期和事件市场预测的延迟结算机制；(6) SFT和RL后训练的集成支持。

Result: 论文提出了OpenFinGym平台的设计与架构，属于系统/平台类论文，未报告具体的基准测试实验结果或智能体性能对比数据。

Conclusion: OpenFinGym通过统一的多任务接口、论文驱动的任务构建、安全的容器化评估和真实交易模拟，为量化金融智能体提供了更贴近真实金融工作流的评估与开发平台，有望推动该领域从碎片化评估走向系统化、金融相关性更强的基准测试范式。

Abstract: Although large language model agents are increasingly applied to quantitative-finance workflows, their evaluation remains fragmented across isolated tasks, while the financial relevance of benchmark tasks is often overlooked. Yet financial workflows are inherently multi-stage, spanning interdependent tasks such as forecasting, strategy construction, risk management, and trading. Existing platforms typically focus on a single task, and can therefore overstate agent competence and fail to reveal weaknesses in generalization, real-market interaction, and financially meaningful decision-making. We introduce OpenFinGym, a unified gym environment for quantitative-finance agent development that covers forecasting, market generation, real-time trading, and fraud detection under a single execution and verification interface. OpenFinGym additionally provides an automated task-construction pipeline that turns quantitative finance publications into executable task packages; a containerised runtime with a host-side verifier service that supports scalable agent rollouts and prevents runtime train-test leakage; a paper trading engine with a low-latency data-stream design; deferred-resolution support for long-horizon and event-market forecasts; and integration for SFT and RL post-training

</details>


### [203] [Instruction Bleed: Cross-Module Interference in Prompt-Composed Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26356)
*Ching-Yu Lin,Yifan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了"组合行为泄漏"（CBL）现象：在由多个提示模块组合而成的智能体系统中，编辑其中一个模块会通过共享上下文窗口无声地影响其他模块的行为，即使它们之间没有显式依赖。通过在部署的作业评估智能体上的实验，发现内容扰动可产生可检测但未达到决策翻转阈值的影响，属于亚阈值干扰。文章贡献了操作化定义、可复用协议与可证伪预测集，确立了跨模块干扰测量作为提示组合式智能体评估的必要环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示组合式智能体系统的从业者反复观察到一种失效模式：修改一个提示模块会悄然改变其他模块的行为，尽管它们之间没有共享变量或可执行依赖。这一现象缺乏正式定义与系统测量方法，需要将其形式化并建立评估协议，以揭示Transformer自注意力机制下模块间非隔离架构带来的隐性干扰风险。

Method: （1）将"组合行为泄漏"（CBL）形式化定义为共享上下文窗口的模块间干扰，归因于Transformer自注意力缺乏模块间形式边界。 （2）设计三通道扰动协议：容量（volume）、内容（content）、形式（form），用于在已部署的作业评估智能体（Claude Sonnet 4.6）上进行144次试验。 （3）扰动非焦点模块，测量对焦点模块的配对效应量（Cohen's d）和决策翻转率。 （4）构建可证伪预测集并进行系统类别表征。

Result: 仅内容通道产生了可检测的配对效应（Cohen's d = 0.63，bootstrap 95% CI排除零），但没有任何推荐决策发生翻转——属于标准QA不可见的"亚阈值"状态。然而，在部署智能体做出的数千次决策中，这种亚阈值效应会复合累积，产生实质性影响。

Conclusion: CBL是一种正交于已知智能体失效轴（对抗注入、认知退化、多智能体故障传播、隐私泄漏）的新型失效模式。作者确立了跨模块干扰测量作为提示组合式智能体评估的必要要求，为后续安全部署提供了可操作的检测框架。

Abstract: Practitioners of prompt-composed agentic systems report a recurring failure mode: editing one prompt module silently shifts the behavior of others despite no shared variable or executable dependency. We formalize this as compositional behavioral leakage (CBL): interference between modules sharing a context window. CBL is enabled by architectural non-isolation: transformer self-attention provides no formal boundary between concatenated modules. We probe CBL on a deployed job-evaluation agent (Claude Sonnet 4.6, 144 trials) through a reusable three-channel protocol that perturbs non-focal modules along volume, content, and form. Only the content channel produces a detectable paired effect (Cohen's d = 0.63, bootstrap 95% CI excluding zero); no recommendation flipped -- a sub-threshold regime invisible to standard QA but compounding across the thousands of decisions a deployed agent makes. CBL is orthogonal to known agent-failure axes (adversarial injection, cognitive degradation, multi-agent fault propagation, privacy leakage). We contribute an operational definition, a reusable protocol, a falsifiable prediction set, and a system-class characterization, establishing cross-module interference measurement as a requirement for prompt-composed agent evaluation.

</details>


### [204] [Accelerating Returns and the Qualitative Engine for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26359)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对库兹韦尔的加速回报理论进行了数学化解读，指出该理论主要适用于执行性和基础设施层面的能力增长，但无法解决科学发现的核心问题——即需要定性推理来识别当前框架的结构性不足并做出概念性转变。ARC-AGI-3结果显示人类与AI在灵活推理上仍存在巨大差距（人类达上限，AI低于1%），论文据此将"科学定性引擎"（QES）定位为弥补这一缺失能力的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 库兹韦尔的加速回报理论是技术讨论中最有影响力的叙事之一，但其局限性在于它只能解释执行性能力的指数增长，无法解释科学发现中关键的定性推理和概念跃迁。作者试图揭示这一理论盲区，并为科学发现的核心困境提供替代框架。

Method: 首先对库兹韦尔的加速回报论题给出简化数学解释；然后论证加速回报仅适用于执行性和基础设施层面的能力，而非真正科学发现所需的定性推理；引用ARC-AGI-3基准测试结果（人类达天花板，前沿AI低于1%）作为经验证据，凸显灵活推理的巨大差距；最后引入哈萨比斯关于人类意义感和选择焦点的论述，将"科学定性引擎"（QES）定位为回应上述缺失能力的解决方案。

Result: 加速回报理论在数学上可以解释技术能力的指数增长，但它不触及科学发现的本质过程。ARC-AGI-3结果量化地证明：人类在灵活推理上仍远超AI系统，差距巨大。这意味着当前AI的进展集中在定量执行能力上，而非科学发现所依赖的定性推理和框架重构能力。

Conclusion: 加速回报理论解释了定量能力的指数增长，但科学发现的核心问题需要"科学定性引擎"（QES）来应对。QES的价值不依赖于AGI何时到来，而在于它将科学发现的"过程本身"视为一种值得保存、组织和传承的人类智慧形式。

Abstract: Ray Kurzweil described a thesis of accelerating returns, which is the most influential narratives in discussions of technological progress. Its central claim is that advances in multiple technological fields, especially compute, artificial intelligence, brain science, and biotechnology, interact in such a way that progress becomes self-amplifying and approximately exponential. This paper gives a simple mathematical interpretation of that claim and then argues that, even if such acceleration is real, it does not by itself resolve the central problem of scientific discovery. The reason is that accelerating returns apply most naturally to executional and infrastructural capability, whereas genuine discovery often depends on a different capacity: qualitative reasoning about when a current framework is structurally inadequate and what conceptual move is needed next. Recent ARC-AGI-3 results sharpen this distinction: humans solve the benchmark at ceiling, whereas frontier AI systems remain below 1%, indicating that the gap between current AI and human flexible reasoning is still very large. At the same time, Demis Hassabis has emphasized that humans must retain their sense of meaning and what they choose to focus their lives on, a reminder that the future of AI is not only a technical forecast but also a question of what forms of human understanding are worth preserving and transmitting. This paper positions the Qualitative Engine for Science (QES) [3] as a response to that missing capacity. In this view, the Kurzweil theory helps explain why quantitative capability may accelerate, while QES addresses the central problem in scientific discovery that acceleration alone does not solve. Its value does not depend on when AGI arrives, but on the fact that the processes of scientific discovery themselves constitute a form of human wisdom worth preserving, organizing, and making accessible.

</details>


### [205] [Narration-of-Thought: Inference-Time Scaffolding for Defeasible Ethical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26366)
*Patrick Cooper,Alvaro Velasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对标准思维链在道德困境推理中出现的"利益相关者坍缩"和"不确定性抑制"两种失效模式，作者提出了"思维叙事"(NoT)提示框架，将推理过程结构化为五段式，无需训练即可大幅降低失效，并通过消融实验、文本梯度优化及多利益相关者辩论扩展验证了框架的有效性与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准思维链（CoT）在处理道德困境时存在两个关键缺陷：一是"利益相关者坍缩"——推理过程中最多只提及一个利益相关方；二是"不确定性抑制"——在做出行动承诺前缺乏对未知因素的明确陈述或保留。这导致模型决策过程不透明、不可靠，需要一种无需额外训练即可改善的推理结构。

Method: 提出**思维叙事（Narration-of-Thought, NoT）**系统提示，将思维链结构化为五个部分：**主角（protagonist）、利益相关者（stakeholders）、两步后果（two-step consequences）、不确定性（uncertainty）、承诺（commitment）**。该方法不增加任何训练、参数或微调。实验在100个DailyDilemmas场景上测试了三个供应商的四个生成器。还设置了**匹配预算的冗长思维链对照**以排除token数量影响，进行了**段落消融实验**以归因各部分的贡献，并采用**文本梯度下降**进一步优化NoT。此外，还将框架扩展至**五轮多利益相关者辩论协议**进行验证。

Result: 1. NoT将利益相关者坍缩率从最高31%降至1%以下，不确定性抑制率从最高72%降至1-24%；2. 匹配预算的冗长CoT对照排除了token消耗量作为活性成分的可能；3. NoT在利益相关者计数上的Cliff's delta效应量优势达+0.79至+0.90，在不确定性评分上达+0.65至+0.93（四个生成器中的三个）；4. 段落消融实验将每项改进归因于对应的子指令；5. 文本梯度下降进一步提升了NoT框架；6. 跨族训练裁判（与生成器不同供应商）在所有测量轴向上优于族内裁判；7. 多利益相关者辩论协议将6%的僵局转化为95%完全共识（校准集），在DailyDilemmas复现中达到100%联合收敛。

Conclusion: NoT通过结构化叙述外部化了每个承诺背后的利益相关者、后果和不确定性，为智能体的可靠部署提供了可审计的推理基底。这种零训练成本的提示工程方法在道德推理的透明性和稳健性方面取得了显著改进。

Abstract: Standard chain-of-thought on moral dilemmas exhibits two failure modes: stakeholder collapse (the trace names at most one party with a stake in the outcome) and uncertainty suppression (no explicit unknowns or hedges before committing to an action). We introduce narration-of-thought (NoT), a system prompt that structures chain-of-thought into five sections: protagonist, stakeholders, two-step consequences, uncertainty, then commitment. NoT adds no training, parameters, or fine-tuning. On 100 DailyDilemmas scenarios across four generators from three vendors, NoT cuts stakeholder collapse from up to 31% to under 1% and uncertainty suppression from up to 72% to 1-24% on every model. A matched-budget verbose-CoT control rules out token spend as the active ingredient; NoT retains Cliff's delta advantages of +0.79 to +0.90 on stakeholder count and +0.65 to +0.93 on uncertainty score for three of four generators, and a section ablation attributes each shift to its specific sub-instruction. Textual-gradient descent initialised at NoT improves the scaffold further; a cross-family training judge (different vendor from the generator) dominates an in-family one on every measured axis. Extended to a five-round multi-stakeholder debate protocol, the scaffold converts a 6% standoff into 95% full consensus on a calibration set and 100% combined convergence on a DailyDilemmas replication. The resulting traces externalise the stakeholders, consequences, and uncertainty grounding each commitment, providing an auditable substrate for dependable agentic deployment.

</details>


### [206] [Geometry-Aware MCTS for Extremal Problems in Combinatorial Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26399)
*Luoning Zhang,Xu Zhuang,Tianhao Wang,Nathan Kaplan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）框架，用于解决组合几何中的极值点配置问题。通过增量更新可行动作空间来严格强制几何约束，并利用几何对称性进行剪枝和批量状态转移，在六个问题中的五个上取得了新的最佳计算结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典精确求解器在组合几何极值问题上面临组合爆炸；标准强化学习和Transformer模型则受困于稀疏奖励的"有效性悬崖"以及二次增长的token消耗限制。因此需要一种既能严格满足几何约束、又具备高效搜索能力的新框架。

Method: 提出几何感知MCTS框架，包含三个核心设计：(1) 通过增量更新可行动作空间来严格强制几何约束，对共线约束（如No-Three-in-Line问题）将约束检查复杂度从O(n³)降至O(n²)；(2) 在节点扩展时利用几何对称性进行规范剪枝以减少分支因子；(3) 采用对称批量状态转移加速发现有前景的配置。

Result: 在6个测试问题中的5个上取得新的最佳计算结果。在Max-N3IL问题上，对82≤n≤119的网格找到了约1.8n大小的配置；在Smallest Complete Set问题上找到了约0.95n大小的配置，为测试网格提供了新的上界。

Conclusion: 几何感知MCTS是一种高度自适应的框架，能够有效发现组合几何中的新型配置，在多个经典问题上突破了现有计算记录，展示了将几何结构知识融入树搜索的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We study certain extremal problems in combinatorial geometry that ask about configurations of points in an $n \times n$ grid that satisfy strict, global geometric constraints. Classical exact solvers suffer from combinatorial explosion for these types of problems, and standard reinforcement learning and transformer-based models struggle with the sparse reward "validity cliff" and quadratic token-consumption limits. To overcome these bottlenecks, we propose a Geometry-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) framework. Our approach strictly enforces geometric constraints through incremental updates to the feasible action space. For constraints about collections of collinear points, like those that occur in the classic No-Three-in-Line problem (Max-N3IL), this mechanism reduces the constraint checking complexity from $O(n^3)$ to $O(n^2)$. To improve search efficiency, we exploit geometric symmetries in two ways: canonical pruning during node expansion to reduce the branching factor, and symmetric batch transitions to accelerate the discovery of promising configurations. We perform extensive experiments and establish new best-known computational results on five out of six of the problems that we considered. Notably, for Max-N3IL we find configurations of size roughly $1.8 n$ for grids of size $82 \le n \le 119$. For the Smallest Complete Set problem, we find configurations of size roughly $0.95 n$, providing new upper bounds within the tested grids. This work establishes Geometry-Aware MCTS as a highly adaptable framework for discovering novel configurations in combinatorial geometry.

</details>


### [207] [When Agents Meet Electric Bus Fleet Operations: Pricing Behavior, Trade-offs, and Policy Implications in an Aggregator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26400)
*Jônatas Augusto Manzolli,Ali Eslami,Luis Miranda-Moreno,Jiangbo Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种智能体聚合框架，将优化调度模型与监督型智能体耦合，用于电动公交车的自适应车队-电网协调。案例研究表明该方法能提升灵活性利用并维持调度可行性，但利润导向配置下存在从公交运营商抽取价值的风险，因此在实际部署中需建立透明、可审计的价值分配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电动公交车队运营涉及服务可靠性、电池荷电状态、充电桩可用性、电价、路线能耗不确定性以及V2G机会等多重因素的持续协调，传统集中优化方法难以适应实时动态变化。本文旨在探索将智能体系统(agentic system)与优化调度模型相结合，以实现自适应、灵活的电动公交车队-电网协调管理。

Method: 提出一个智能体聚合框架(agentic aggregator framework)，核心包含：(1) 基于优化的电动公交车调度模型，确保路线、充电桩、电池及V2G交互的物理可行性；(2) 监督型智能体层，负责扰动检测、电价自适应调整和调度方案评估。智能体层解读运行条件变化，按需触发实时重优化，并定义聚合商与公共交通运营商之间的灵活性价值分配规则。通过真实车场案例仿真，在日前和实时运行场景下，评估利润导向和运营导向两种协调模式在服务延误、路线能耗偏差、电价冲击及复合扰动下的表现。

Result: (1) 智能体聚合能够通过维持可行调度、选择性激活重优化、改善充电和V2G灵活性利用来支持自适应车队-电网协调；(2) 但同时揭示了一个关键权衡：以利润为导向的定价配置下，降低运营复杂度的同一个智能体能力可能从公交运营商处抽取价值。

Conclusion: 智能体聚合器在管理电动公交车V2G运营方面具有实用潜力，但在公共交通车队场景中的部署需要透明的协调模式、可审计的电价设定机制以及明确的价值分配规则，以确保公平性和公共利益的维护。

Abstract: Agentic systems are changing how complex operational tasks are coordinated, introducing a new paradigm for connecting heterogeneous data sources and automating processes. Electric bus fleets provide a relevant test case. Their operation requires continuous coordination between service reliability, battery state-of-charge, charger availability, electricity prices, route-energy uncertainty, and vehicle-to-grid (V2G) opportunities. This paper proposes an agentic aggregator framework that streamlines this decision environment by coupling an optimization-based electric bus scheduling model with supervisory agents for disturbance detection, tariff adaptation, and schedule evaluation. The optimization core enforces physical feasibility across routes, chargers, batteries, and V2G exchanges, while the agentic layer interprets changing operating conditions, triggers real-time re-optimization when needed, and defines how flexibility value is allocated between the aggregator and the public transport operator (PTO). A realistic depot case study evaluates day-ahead and real-time operations under profit-based and operation-based coordination modes, considering service delays, route-energy deviations, electricity price shocks, and combined disturbances. The results show that agentic aggregation can support adaptive fleet-grid coordination by maintaining feasible schedules, activating re-optimization selectively, and improving the use of charging and V2G flexibility. However, they also reveal a critical trade-off: the same agentic capability that reduces operational complexity can extract value from the PTO when configured around profit-oriented pricing. These findings suggest that agentic aggregators can become useful for managing electric bus V2G operations, but their deployment in public-fleet contexts requires transparent coordination modes, auditable tariff-setting, and explicit value-sharing rules.

</details>


### [208] [Unbiased Canonical Set-Valued Oracles Via Lattice Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26418)
*Jobst Heitzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究"预言型AI"面临的自我指涉问题：一旦预言被知晓并据此行动，原概率即被改变。作者提出一种基于Knaster-Tarski不动点定理的方案，以可信集（credal set）替代单点概率，寻找同时满足无偏性与自洽性的最小不动点，并证明其存在性、非空性及在非施为性问题下退化为经典点估计等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非智能体式的"神谕"AI在估计未来事件概率时，其输出一旦被学习并引发行动，就会改变它本该报告的概率。Scientist AI方案主张只回答反事实问题（假设答案无影响），但作者指出，一旦答案被知晓，反事实前提即告失效，答案随即变得无关。因此需要一种能直面自我指涉困境的新范式。

Method: 将神谕的输出从单点概率推广为可信集（credal set），要求其同时满足"无偏性"（以恰当条件化定义）和"自洽性"（考虑到答案被学习后对概率的影响）。在闭可信集构成的完备格上，利用Knaster-Tarski不动点定理构造一个保序算子，取其最小不动点作为规范答案；另一变体则取包含所有自洽点估计的最小不动点。构造纯格论，可自然推广到任意随机变量的条件律。

Result: 证明规范可信集的存在性、自洽性及非空性；在非施为性（non-performative）问题下，构造退化为经典单点概率答案；对于二元事件，在自然的"壳-因子化"假设下，规范答案为一区间。框架自然推广至任意随机变量X，以条件律替代条件概率。

Conclusion: 该方案为自我指涉性预言问题提供了严格的数学基础，但尚存开放问题，包括区间表征在推广至一般随机变量时是否仍然成立。

Abstract: A non-agentic "oracle" AI that estimates probabilities of future events faces a self-reference problem: once its answer is learned and acted upon, it can change the very probability it was asked to report. One response, advocated for the Scientist AI programme, is to ask only counterfactual questions, evaluated as if the answer had no influence. We observe that such answers tend to become irrelevant the moment they are learned, precisely because their premise is then false. We therefore explore a self-referential alternative in which the oracle reports not a single probability but a credal set that is simultaneously unbiased and self-consistent with the consequences of being learned. The naive self-consistency requirement is satisfied by too many sets (including the useless answer $[0,1]$), so the problem is to single out a canonical, nontrivial member. We do so with the Knaster--Tarski fixed-point theorem on the complete lattice of closed credal sets, taking the least fixed point of a suitably defined isotone operator; a variant instead reports the least fixed point that contains every self-consistent point estimate. We prove existence, self-consistency, and nonemptiness, show that the construction collapses to the classical point answer for non-performative questions, and that for a binary event the canonical answer is, under a natural hull-factoring assumption, an interval. The development is purely lattice-theoretic and extends unchanged from a binary event $B$ to an arbitrary random variable $X$, with $P(B\mid A,C)$ replaced by the conditional law $\mathcal{L}(X\mid A,C)$. We close with open questions, including whether the interval characterization itself survives that generalization.

</details>


### [209] [Estimating Uncertainty in Classifier Performance with Applications to Large Language Models and Nested Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26422)
*Kylie Anglin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了社会科学文本分类中性能指标（如召回率、精确率、F1）置信区间的多种估计方法，发现在小样本、低频构念和文本嵌套于个体的典型条件下，默认方法（Wald区间、基本百分位bootstrap）表现最差。推荐使用Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson区间以及一种新颖的伪计数正则化bootstrap方法，并指出在嵌套数据中需对有效样本量和自由度进行校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学研究者日益依赖文本分类来衡量构念，但性能指标（召回率、精确率等）的不确定性度量报告不一致，且常用的置信区间估计方法在小样本或高性能条件下并不适用，亟需系统评估并为该领域提供方法指导。

Method: 通过模拟研究，系统比较多种置信区间方法（Wald、Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson、基本百分位bootstrap、聚类bootstrap、分层bootstrap及新提出的伪计数正则化bootstrap）在典型社会科学文本分类条件下的覆盖率和准确性。条件涵盖：小到中等样本量、低频构念、文本嵌套于个体层级结构。

Result: 默认的Wald区间和基本百分位bootstrap准确性最差，覆盖率常远低于名义95%水平。Agresti-Coull、Wilson、Clopper-Pearson及伪计数正则化bootstrap（尤其适用于F1）显著提升了准确性。在嵌套数据下，分析区间需同时对有效N和自由度进行校正；分层bootstrap在个体产生中等数量文本时优于聚类bootstrap，但在个体仅产生少量文本时趋于过度保守。

Conclusion: 通过为领域提供适当的区间估计方法指导，本文旨在提升机器学习应用的透明度，并鼓励在研究设计阶段对验证样本量给予更多关注。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use text classification--supervised models or large language models--to measure constructs from natural language, providing metrics such as recall and precision as evidence of their validity. Yet, though these metrics are point estimates subject to sampling variation, measures of uncertainty are inconsistently reported alongside them. Further, when they are reported, they are often estimated with methods that are not appropriate when relevant labelled datasets are small or performance is high. To increase and improve confidence interval reporting in the field, this paper evaluates confidence interval methods for performance metrics under conditions typical of social science text classification: small to moderate sample sizes, infrequent constructs, and texts nested within individuals. Across simulations, default methods such as the Wald interval and the basic percentile bootstrap are the least accurate, with coverage sometimes far below the nominal 95% level. Accuracy is improved with the use of Agresti-Coull, Wilson, Clopper-Pearson, and a novel pseudo-count regularized bootstrap (which is particularly relevant to the calculation of F1). When texts are nested within individuals, we demonstrate that adjustment for both effective N and the appropriate degrees of freedom is necessary for producing accurate analytic intervals. Among bootstrap intervals, the hierarchical bootstrap is more accurate than the cluster bootstrap when individuals produce a moderate number of texts but overly conservative when individuals produce only a few. By providing guidance to the field on appropriate interval estimation, we aim to improve the transparency of machine learning applications, and to encourage greater attention to the validation sample size at the design stage.

</details>


### [210] [Data-driven Machine Learning Cannot Reach Symbolic-level Logical Reasoning -- The Limit of the Scaling Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.26454)
*Tiansi Dong,Mateja Jamnik,Pietro Liò*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析和实验证明：监督深度学习受限于训练数据的区分能力不足及端到端映射带来的内在矛盾，无法实现符号级的三段论推理；即使ChatGPT在表面形式上可达100%准确率，其推理仍缺乏严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨缩放定律在逻辑推理中的极限：既然球面神经网络已能在无训练数据条件下达到符号级三段论推理，那么数据驱动的监督学习能否通过增加数据和训练时间达到同等水平？
